@@@FILE app/src/main/java/com/mohit/screenreader/ApiKeyActivity.kt lines=57
package com.mohit.screenreader

import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Context
import android.os.Bundle
import android.widget.Button
import android.widget.EditText
import android.widget.TextView

class ApiKeyActivity : Activity() {

    private lateinit var status: TextView
    private lateinit var edit: EditText

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_api_key)
        status = findViewById(R.id.txtKeyStatus)
        edit = findViewById(R.id.edtApiKey)
        showStatus()

        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnSaveKey).setOnClickListener { save() }
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnRemoveKey).setOnClickListener { remove() }
    }

    private fun prefs() = getSharedPreferences("screen_reader_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    private fun showStatus() {
        val key = prefs().getString("gemini_api_key", null)
        status.text = if (key.isNullOrEmpty()) {
            "No API key is saved yet."
        } else {
            "An API key is saved, ending in " + key.takeLast(4) + ". Enter a new one to replace it."
        }
    }

    private fun tell(message: String) {
        status.text = message
        status.announceForAccessibility(message)
    }

    private fun save() {
        val key = edit.text.toString().trim()
        if (key.isEmpty()) {
            tell("The box is empty. Paste your API key first.")
            return
        }
        prefs().edit().putString("gemini_api_key", key).apply()
        edit.setText("")
        tell("API key saved, ending in " + key.takeLast(4) + ".")
    }

    private fun remove() {
        prefs().edit().remove("gemini_api_key").apply()
        tell("API key removed.")
    }
}
@@@FILE app/src/main/java/com/mohit/screenreader/AutoClickEditActivity.kt lines=81
package com.mohit.screenreader

import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Context
import android.os.Bundle
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.widget.Button
import android.widget.EditText
import android.widget.TextView

class AutoClickEditActivity : Activity() {

    private var index = -1
    private lateinit var nameBox: EditText
    private lateinit var stepsBox: EditText
    private lateinit var status: TextView

    private fun prefs() = getSharedPreferences("screen_reader_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_autoclick_edit)
        nameBox = findViewById(R.id.edtMacroName)
        stepsBox = findViewById(R.id.edtMacroSteps)
        status = findViewById(R.id.txtMacroStatus)
        index = intent.getIntExtra("index", -1)

        val delete = findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnDeleteMacro)
        if (index >= 0) {
            val list = AutoClickStore.load(prefs())
            if (index < list.size) {
                nameBox.setText(list[index].name)
                stepsBox.setText(list[index].steps.joinToString("\n"))
            } else {
                index = -1
            }
        }
        if (index < 0) delete.isEnabled = false

        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnSaveMacro).setOnClickListener { save() }
        delete.setOnClickListener { remove() }
    }

    private fun tell(message: String, thenFinish: Boolean) {
        status.text = message
        status.announceForAccessibility(message)
        if (thenFinish) Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed({ finish() }, 1800)
    }

    private fun save() {
        val name = nameBox.text.toString().trim()
        val steps = stepsBox.text.toString().split("\n").map { it.trim() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
        if (name.isEmpty()) {
            tell("Please give the function a name.", false)
            return
        }
        if (steps.isEmpty()) {
            tell("Please paste at least one item to click.", false)
            return
        }
        val list = AutoClickStore.load(prefs())
        if (index >= 0 && index < list.size) {
            list[index] = Macro(name, steps)
        } else {
            list.add(Macro(name, steps))
        }
        AutoClickStore.save(prefs(), list)
        tell("Saved " + name + " with " + steps.size + (if (steps.size == 1) " step." else " steps."), true)
    }

    private fun remove() {
        val list = AutoClickStore.load(prefs())
        if (index >= 0 && index < list.size) {
            val name = list[index].name
            list.removeAt(index)
            AutoClickStore.save(prefs(), list)
            tell("Deleted " + name + ".", true)
        }
    }
}
@@@FILE app/src/main/java/com/mohit/screenreader/AutoClickListActivity.kt lines=44
package com.mohit.screenreader

import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.os.Bundle
import android.widget.ArrayAdapter
import android.widget.Button
import android.widget.ListView

class AutoClickListActivity : Activity() {

    private lateinit var list: ListView
    private val rows = mutableListOf<String>()
    private lateinit var adapter: ArrayAdapter<String>

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_autoclick_list)
        list = findViewById(R.id.listMacros)
        adapter = ArrayAdapter(this, android.R.layout.simple_list_item_1, rows)
        list.adapter = adapter
        list.setOnItemClickListener { _, _, position, _ ->
            val i = Intent(this, AutoClickEditActivity::class.java)
            i.putExtra("index", position)
            startActivity(i)
        }
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnNewMacro).setOnClickListener {
            val i = Intent(this, AutoClickEditActivity::class.java)
            i.putExtra("index", -1)
            startActivity(i)
        }
    }

    override fun onResume() {
        super.onResume()
        val prefs = getSharedPreferences("screen_reader_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        rows.clear()
        for (m in AutoClickStore.load(prefs)) {
            rows.add(m.name + ", " + m.steps.size + (if (m.steps.size == 1) " step" else " steps"))
        }
        adapter.notifyDataSetChanged()
    }
}
@@@FILE app/src/main/java/com/mohit/screenreader/Customization.kt lines=82
package com.mohit.screenreader

import android.content.SharedPreferences
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject

// Items of the Screen Reader Menu and the reading controls that can be switched on or off.
// The user stores only what is switched OFF, so new items added in future updates
// appear automatically (switched on).
object MenuConfig {
    val menuItems: List<Pair<String, String>> = listOf(
        "copy" to "Copy",
        "append" to "Append, add to what was copied before",
        "cut" to "Cut",
        "paste" to "Paste",
        "select" to "Select",
        "clear" to "Clear edit box",
        "ai" to "AI menu",
        "extensions" to "Extensions",
        "translate" to "Translate",
        "assistant" to "Voice assistant"
    )

    val readingItems: List<Pair<String, String>> = listOf(
        "character" to "Character",
        "word" to "Word",
        "line" to "Line",
        "paragraph" to "Paragraph",
        "actions" to "Actions",
        "slider" to "Slider",
        "heading" to "Heading",
        "link" to "Link",
        "editbox" to "Edit box"
    )

    fun disabled(prefs: SharedPreferences, kind: String): Set<String> =
        prefs.getStringSet("disabled_" + kind, emptySet()) ?: emptySet()
}

// Built-in extensions. Anything added here (or any auto click function the user creates)
// automatically shows up in the list of functions a gesture can run.
object Extensions {
    val builtin: List<Pair<String, String>> = listOf(
        "yt_skip" to "Skip ads on YouTube"
    )
}

class Macro(var name: String, var steps: List<String>)

// Auto click functions: a name plus a list of on-screen items to click, in order.
object AutoClickStore {
    fun load(prefs: SharedPreferences): MutableList<Macro> {
        val out = mutableListOf<Macro>()
        val s = prefs.getString("autoclick_functions", null) ?: return out
        try {
            val arr = JSONArray(s)
            for (i in 0 until arr.length()) {
                val o = arr.getJSONObject(i)
                val stepsArr = o.getJSONArray("steps")
                val steps = mutableListOf<String>()
                for (k in 0 until stepsArr.length()) steps.add(stepsArr.getString(k))
                out.add(Macro(o.getString("name"), steps))
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // ignore damaged data
        }
        return out
    }

    fun save(prefs: SharedPreferences, list: List<Macro>) {
        val arr = JSONArray()
        for (m in list) {
            val o = JSONObject()
            o.put("name", m.name)
            val st = JSONArray()
            for (x in m.steps) st.put(x)
            o.put("steps", st)
            arr.put(o)
        }
        prefs.edit().putString("autoclick_functions", arr.toString()).apply()
    }
}
@@@FILE app/src/main/java/com/mohit/screenreader/CustomizeListActivity.kt lines=41
package com.mohit.screenreader

import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Context
import android.os.Bundle
import android.widget.ArrayAdapter
import android.widget.ListView
import android.widget.TextView

// Tick / untick the items of the Screen Reader Menu ("menu") or the reading controls ("reading").
class CustomizeListActivity : Activity() {

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_customize_list)
        val kind = intent.getStringExtra("kind") ?: "menu"
        val items = if (kind == "menu") MenuConfig.menuItems else MenuConfig.readingItems
        val prefs = getSharedPreferences("screen_reader_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val key = "disabled_" + kind

        val info = findViewById<TextView>(R.id.txtCustomizeInfo)
        info.text = if (kind == "menu") {
            "Screen reader menu. Tick the options you want to keep. Unticked options are hidden. New options added in future updates appear ticked automatically. Close menu is always available."
        } else {
            "Reading controls. Tick the controls you want to keep. Unticked controls are skipped when you change reading control. New controls added in future updates appear ticked automatically. Normal item is always available."
        }

        val list = findViewById<ListView>(R.id.listCustomize)
        list.choiceMode = ListView.CHOICE_MODE_MULTIPLE
        list.adapter = ArrayAdapter(this, android.R.layout.simple_list_item_multiple_choice, items.map { it.second })
        val off = prefs.getStringSet(key, emptySet()) ?: emptySet()
        items.forEachIndexed { i, it -> list.setItemChecked(i, !off.contains(it.first)) }

        list.setOnItemClickListener { _, _, position, _ ->
            val id = items[position].first
            val set = HashSet(prefs.getStringSet(key, emptySet()) ?: emptySet())
            if (list.isItemChecked(position)) set.remove(id) else set.add(id)
            prefs.edit().putStringSet(key, set).apply()
        }
    }
}

@@@FILE app/src/main/java/com/mohit/screenreader/GamingAppsActivity.kt lines=82
package com.mohit.screenreader

import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.ApplicationInfo
import android.os.Bundle
import android.widget.ArrayAdapter
import android.widget.ListView
import android.widget.TextView

class GamingAppsActivity : Activity() {

    private class Row(val pkg: String, val label: String, val auto: Boolean)

    private val rows = mutableListOf<Row>()
    private lateinit var list: ListView
    private lateinit var status: TextView

    private fun prefs() = getSharedPreferences("screen_reader_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_gaming_apps)
        list = findViewById(R.id.listApps)
        status = findViewById(R.id.txtGamingStatus)
        status.text = "Loading apps..."

        Thread {
            val pm = packageManager
            val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_MAIN).addCategory(Intent.CATEGORY_LAUNCHER)
            val seen = HashSet<String>()
            val found = mutableListOf<Row>()
            for (ri in pm.queryIntentActivities(intent, 0)) {
                val pkg = ri.activityInfo.packageName
                if (pkg == packageName || !seen.add(pkg)) continue
                val label = ri.loadLabel(pm).toString()
                val auto = try {
                    pm.getApplicationInfo(pkg, 0).category == ApplicationInfo.CATEGORY_GAME
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    false
                }
                found.add(Row(pkg, label, auto))
            }
            found.sortBy { it.label.lowercase() }
            runOnUiThread { show(found) }
        }.start()
    }

    private fun show(found: List<Row>) {
        rows.clear()
        rows.addAll(found)
        val p = prefs()
        val on = p.getStringSet("gaming_on", emptySet()) ?: emptySet()
        val off = p.getStringSet("gaming_off", emptySet()) ?: emptySet()

        val labels = rows.map { if (it.auto) it.label + ", detected as a game" else it.label }
        val adapter = ArrayAdapter(this, android.R.layout.simple_list_item_multiple_choice, labels)
        list.choiceMode = ListView.CHOICE_MODE_MULTIPLE
        list.adapter = adapter
        rows.forEachIndexed { i, r ->
            val checked = on.contains(r.pkg) || (r.auto && !off.contains(r.pkg))
            list.setItemChecked(i, checked)
        }
        list.setOnItemClickListener { _, _, position, _ ->
            val r = rows[position]
            val nowChecked = list.isItemChecked(position)
            val sp = prefs()
            val newOn = HashSet(sp.getStringSet("gaming_on", emptySet()) ?: emptySet())
            val newOff = HashSet(sp.getStringSet("gaming_off", emptySet()) ?: emptySet())
            if (nowChecked) {
                newOn.add(r.pkg)
                newOff.remove(r.pkg)
            } else {
                newOff.add(r.pkg)
                newOn.remove(r.pkg)
            }
            sp.edit().putStringSet("gaming_on", newOn).putStringSet("gaming_off", newOff).apply()
        }
        status.text = "Tick the apps that should use gaming mode. Apps detected as games are already ticked; untick one to turn it off."
    }
}
@@@FILE app/src/main/java/com/mohit/screenreader/GeminiClient.kt lines=249
package com.mohit.screenreader

import android.content.Context
import java.net.HttpURLConnection
import java.net.URL
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject

// Text-only Gemini calls with automatic fallback between models.
object GeminiClient {

    class Result(val ok: Boolean, val text: String)

    private class Attempt(val ok: Boolean, val text: String, val next: Boolean, val busy: Boolean)

    private val known = listOf(
        "gemini-2.5-flash",
        "gemini-2.5-flash-lite",
        "gemini-flash-latest",
        "gemini-flash-lite-latest",
        "gemini-2.0-flash",
        "gemini-2.0-flash-lite"
    )

    val languages = listOf(
        "English", "Hindi", "Urdu", "Punjabi", "Marathi", "Gujarati", "Bengali", "Tamil", "Telugu",
        "Kannada", "Malayalam", "Odia", "Assamese", "Nepali", "Sanskrit", "Spanish", "French", "German",
        "Arabic", "Chinese", "Japanese", "Korean", "Russian", "Portuguese", "Italian", "Indonesian", "Turkish"
    )

    private fun tryModel(model: String, key: String, prompt: String): Attempt {
        try {
            val url = URL("https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/" + model + ":generateContent")
            val conn = url.openConnection() as HttpURLConnection
            conn.requestMethod = "POST"
            conn.connectTimeout = 20000
            conn.readTimeout = 60000
            conn.doOutput = true
            conn.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json")
            conn.setRequestProperty("x-goog-api-key", key)
            val parts = JSONArray().put(JSONObject().put("text", prompt))
            val body = JSONObject().put("contents", JSONArray().put(JSONObject().put("parts", parts)))
            conn.outputStream.use { it.write(body.toString().toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)) }

            val code = conn.responseCode
            val stream = if (code in 200..299) conn.inputStream else conn.errorStream
            val resp = stream?.bufferedReader()?.use { it.readText() } ?: ""
            conn.disconnect()

            if (code in 200..299) {
                val out = StringBuilder()
                val cands = JSONObject(resp).optJSONArray("candidates")
                val partsOut = cands?.optJSONObject(0)?.optJSONObject("content")?.optJSONArray("parts")
                if (partsOut != null) {
                    for (i in 0 until partsOut.length()) {
                        out.append(partsOut.optJSONObject(i)?.optString("text", "") ?: "")
                    }
                }
                val text = out.toString().trim()
                if (text.isNotEmpty()) return Attempt(true, text, false, false)
                return Attempt(false, "The AI did not return any text. The content may have been blocked.", true, false)
            }
            val apiMsg = try {
                JSONObject(resp).getJSONObject("error").getString("message")
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                ""
            }
            return when (code) {
                400 -> if (apiMsg.contains("API key", true)) {
                    Attempt(false, "Your API key is not valid. Please check it in the app.", false, false)
                } else {
                    Attempt(false, "The request was rejected. " + apiMsg, true, false)
                }
                401, 403 -> Attempt(false, "Your API key was rejected. Please check it in the app.", false, false)
                404 -> Attempt(false, "AI model " + model + " is not available.", true, false)
                429 -> Attempt(false, "Quota exceeded or too many requests.", true, true)
                500, 502, 503, 504 -> Attempt(false, "The AI service is busy.", true, true)
                else -> Attempt(false, "AI service error " + code + ". " + apiMsg, true, false)
            }
        } catch (e: java.net.UnknownHostException) {
            return Attempt(false, "No internet connection.", false, false)
        } catch (e: java.net.SocketTimeoutException) {
            return Attempt(false, "The AI request timed out.", true, true)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            return Attempt(false, "Network error. Please try again.", true, false)
        }
    }

    // Must be called from a background thread.
    fun generate(context: Context, prompt: String): Result {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("screen_reader_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val key = prefs.getString("gemini_api_key", null)?.trim()
        if (key.isNullOrEmpty()) {
            return Result(false, "No API key found. Open the screen reader app and choose Add API key first.")
        }
        val lastGood = prefs.getString("gemini_last_model", null)
        val models = if (lastGood != null && known.contains(lastGood)) {
            listOf(lastGood) + known.filter { it != lastGood }
        } else {
            known
        }
        var lastError = "Unknown error."
        var sawBusy = false
        for (round in 0 until 2) {
            for (model in models) {
                val a = tryModel(model, key, prompt)
                if (a.ok) {
                    prefs.edit().putString("gemini_last_model", model).apply()
                    return Result(true, a.text)
                }
                lastError = a.text
                if (a.busy) sawBusy = true
                if (!a.next) return Result(false, a.text)
            }
            if (round == 0 && sawBusy) {
                try {
                    Thread.sleep(2500)
                } catch (e: InterruptedException) {
                    break
                }
            } else {
                break
            }
        }
        if (sawBusy) return Result(false, "All AI models are busy or over quota right now. Please try again in a minute.")
        return Result(false, lastError)
    }

    // ---- Camera pictures (live object detection and scene description) ----

    private val liveModels = listOf(
        "gemini-2.5-flash-lite",
        "gemini-2.5-flash",
        "gemini-flash-lite-latest",
        "gemini-2.0-flash-lite",
        "gemini-2.0-flash"
    )

    private val sceneModels = listOf(
        "gemini-2.5-flash",
        "gemini-2.5-flash-lite",
        "gemini-flash-latest",
        "gemini-2.0-flash",
        "gemini-2.0-flash-lite"
    )

    private fun tryImage(model: String, key: String, prompt: String, imageB64: String, maxTokens: Int, fast: Boolean): Attempt {
        try {
            val url = URL("https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/" + model + ":generateContent")
            val conn = url.openConnection() as HttpURLConnection
            conn.requestMethod = "POST"
            conn.connectTimeout = if (fast) 8000 else 20000
            conn.readTimeout = if (fast) 20000 else 60000
            conn.doOutput = true
            conn.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json")
            conn.setRequestProperty("x-goog-api-key", key)
            val inline = JSONObject().put("mime_type", "image/jpeg").put("data", imageB64)
            val parts = JSONArray()
                .put(JSONObject().put("text", prompt))
                .put(JSONObject().put("inline_data", inline))
            val gen = JSONObject().put("maxOutputTokens", maxTokens).put("temperature", 0.2)
            // Answer straight away without a long "thinking" phase (only the 2.5 models know this setting).
            if (model.startsWith("gemini-2.5")) {
                gen.put("thinkingConfig", JSONObject().put("thinkingBudget", 0))
            }
            val body = JSONObject()
                .put("contents", JSONArray().put(JSONObject().put("parts", parts)))
                .put("generationConfig", gen)
            conn.outputStream.use { it.write(body.toString().toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)) }

            val code = conn.responseCode
            val stream = if (code in 200..299) conn.inputStream else conn.errorStream
            val resp = stream?.bufferedReader()?.use { it.readText() } ?: ""
            conn.disconnect()

            if (code in 200..299) {
                val out = StringBuilder()
                val cands = JSONObject(resp).optJSONArray("candidates")
                val partsOut = cands?.optJSONObject(0)?.optJSONObject("content")?.optJSONArray("parts")
                if (partsOut != null) {
                    for (i in 0 until partsOut.length()) {
                        out.append(partsOut.optJSONObject(i)?.optString("text", "") ?: "")
                    }
                }
                val text = out.toString().replace("*", "").replace("#", "").trim()
                if (text.isNotEmpty()) return Attempt(true, text, false, false)
                return Attempt(false, "The AI did not describe this picture.", true, false)
            }
            val apiMsg = try {
                JSONObject(resp).getJSONObject("error").getString("message")
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                ""
            }
            return when (code) {
                400 -> if (apiMsg.contains("API key", true)) {
                    Attempt(false, "Your API key is not valid. Please check it in the app.", false, false)
                } else {
                    Attempt(false, "The request was rejected. " + apiMsg, true, false)
                }
                401, 403 -> Attempt(false, "Your API key was rejected. Please check it in the app.", false, false)
                404 -> Attempt(false, "AI model " + model + " is not available.", true, false)
                429 -> Attempt(false, "Too many requests.", true, true)
                500, 502, 503, 504 -> Attempt(false, "The AI service is busy.", true, true)
                else -> Attempt(false, "AI service error " + code + ". " + apiMsg, true, false)
            }
        } catch (e: java.net.UnknownHostException) {
            return Attempt(false, "No internet connection.", false, false)
        } catch (e: java.net.SocketTimeoutException) {
            return Attempt(false, "The AI request timed out.", true, true)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            return Attempt(false, "Network error. Please try again.", true, false)
        }
    }

    class ImageResult(val ok: Boolean, val text: String, val busy: Boolean, val fatal: Boolean)

    // Must be called from a background thread. fast = short live answers; otherwise a full description.
    fun describeImage(context: Context, prompt: String, imageB64: String, fast: Boolean, maxTokens: Int): ImageResult {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("screen_reader_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val key = prefs.getString("gemini_api_key", null)?.trim()
        if (key.isNullOrEmpty()) {
            return ImageResult(false, "No API key found. Open the screen reader app and choose Add API key first.", false, true)
        }
        val list = if (fast) liveModels else sceneModels
        val prefName = if (fast) "gemini_last_live_model" else "gemini_last_scene_model"
        val lastGood = prefs.getString(prefName, null)
        val models = if (lastGood != null && list.contains(lastGood)) {
            listOf(lastGood) + list.filter { it != lastGood }
        } else {
            list
        }
        var lastError = "Unknown error."
        var sawBusy = false
        for (model in models) {
            val a = tryImage(model, key, prompt, imageB64, maxTokens, fast)
            if (a.ok) {
                prefs.edit().putString(prefName, model).apply()
                return ImageResult(true, a.text, false, false)
            }
            lastError = a.text
            if (a.busy) sawBusy = true
            if (!a.next) return ImageResult(false, a.text, false, true)
            // For the live mode one busy answer is enough: slow down instead of trying every model.
            if (fast && a.busy) break
        }
        return ImageResult(false, if (sawBusy) "Too many requests or the AI is busy." else lastError, sawBusy, false)
    }
}

@@@FILE app/src/main/java/com/mohit/screenreader/CameraLiveActivity.kt lines=503
package com.mohit.screenreader

import android.Manifest
import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.app.Activity
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.graphics.ImageFormat
import android.graphics.Matrix
import android.graphics.Rect
import android.graphics.YuvImage
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraCaptureSession
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraCharacteristics
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraDevice
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraManager
import android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest
import android.media.Image
import android.media.ImageReader
import android.os.Bundle
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.HandlerThread
import android.os.Looper
import android.util.Base64
import android.util.Size
import android.view.Gravity
import android.view.Surface
import android.view.WindowManager
import android.widget.FrameLayout
import android.widget.TextView
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream
import java.util.concurrent.ArrayBlockingQueue
import java.util.concurrent.Executors
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean

// Opens the back camera without showing a picture and keeps taking small snapshots of what the
// camera sees (preview frames, no photo is taken), sends them to Gemini and speaks the answer.
//   mode "objects": short live reports of what is in front of you, again and again.
//   mode "scene":   one detailed description; double tap to describe again.
class CameraLiveActivity : Activity() {

    private class Frame(val b64: String, val sig: IntArray)

    private lateinit var status: TextView
    private val ui = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private val camThread = HandlerThread("camera-frames")
    private lateinit var camHandler: Handler
    private val worker = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()

    private var camera: CameraDevice? = null
    private var session: CameraCaptureSession? = null
    private var reader: ImageReader? = null
    private var sensorOrientation = 90

    private var mode = "objects"
    @Volatile private var running = false
    @Volatile private var paused = false
    @Volatile private var describeAgain = false
    private val wantFrame = AtomicBoolean(false)
    private val frames = ArrayBlockingQueue<Frame>(1)
    @Volatile private var targetLongSide = 640
    @Volatile private var jpegQuality = 65

    private var lastReport = ""
    private var lastSig: IntArray? = null
    private var lastUnclearAt = 0L
    private var lastSlowAt = 0L

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        mode = intent?.getStringExtra("mode") ?: "objects"
        window.addFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON)

        val root = FrameLayout(this)
        status = TextView(this)
        status.textSize = 22f
        status.gravity = Gravity.CENTER
        status.setPadding(48, 48, 48, 48)
        status.isClickable = true
        status.isFocusable = true
        status.setOnClickListener { onTapped() }
        root.addView(status, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
            FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT))
        setContentView(root)
        setStatus(if (mode == "scene") "Scene description. Starting the camera."
        else "Live object detection. Starting the camera.")

        camThread.start()
        camHandler = Handler(camThread.looper)

        if (checkSelfPermission(Manifest.permission.CAMERA) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
            openCamera()
        } else {
            say("Camera permission is needed. Please allow it.", true)
            requestPermissions(arrayOf(Manifest.permission.CAMERA), 7)
        }
    }

    override fun onRequestPermissionsResult(requestCode: Int, permissions: Array<out String>, grantResults: IntArray) {
        super.onRequestPermissionsResult(requestCode, permissions, grantResults)
        if (grantResults.isNotEmpty() && grantResults[0] == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
            openCamera()
        } else {
            say("Camera permission was not given. Closing.", true)
            finish()
        }
    }

    private fun setStatus(text: String) {
        status.text = text
        status.contentDescription = text
    }

    private fun say(text: String, flush: Boolean) {
        MyScreenReaderService.instance?.speakExternal(text, flush)
    }

    private fun onTapped() {
        if (mode == "scene") {
            describeAgain = true
        } else {
            paused = !paused
            val t = if (paused) "Paused. Double tap to continue." else "Continuing."
            setStatus(t)
            say(t, true)
        }
    }

    // ---------------- Camera ----------------

    @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
    private fun openCamera() {
        try {
            val mgr = getSystemService(CAMERA_SERVICE) as CameraManager
            val id = mgr.cameraIdList.firstOrNull {
                mgr.getCameraCharacteristics(it).get(CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING) == CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING_BACK
            } ?: mgr.cameraIdList.firstOrNull()
            if (id == null) {
                say("This phone has no camera that can be used.", true)
                finish()
                return
            }
            val ch = mgr.getCameraCharacteristics(id)
            sensorOrientation = ch.get(CameraCharacteristics.SENSOR_ORIENTATION) ?: 90
            val map = ch.get(CameraCharacteristics.SCALER_STREAM_CONFIGURATION_MAP)
            val sizes = map?.getOutputSizes(ImageFormat.YUV_420_888)
            val size = pickSize(sizes)
            val r = ImageReader.newInstance(size.width, size.height, ImageFormat.YUV_420_888, 2)
            r.setOnImageAvailableListener({ rd -> onImage(rd) }, camHandler)
            reader = r
            mgr.openCamera(id, object : CameraDevice.StateCallback() {
                override fun onOpened(cam: CameraDevice) {
                    camera = cam
                    startSession(cam, r)
                }

                override fun onDisconnected(cam: CameraDevice) {
                    cam.close()
                    camera = null
                    ui.post { say("The camera was disconnected.", true); finish() }
                }

                override fun onError(cam: CameraDevice, error: Int) {
                    cam.close()
                    camera = null
                    ui.post { say("The camera could not be opened. Another app may be using it.", true); finish() }
                }
            }, camHandler)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            say("The camera could not be opened.", true)
            finish()
        }
    }

    private fun pickSize(sizes: Array<Size>?): Size {
        if (sizes == null || sizes.isEmpty()) return Size(640, 480)
        // Large enough to be sharp, small enough to be fast: biggest size up to 1280 wide.
        val ok = sizes.filter { maxOf(it.width, it.height) <= 1280 }
        val best = ok.maxByOrNull { it.width.toLong() * it.height }
        return best ?: sizes.minByOrNull { it.width.toLong() * it.height }!!
    }

    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
    private fun startSession(cam: CameraDevice, r: ImageReader) {
        try {
            cam.createCaptureSession(listOf(r.surface), object : CameraCaptureSession.StateCallback() {
                override fun onConfigured(s: CameraCaptureSession) {
                    session = s
                    try {
                        val req = cam.createCaptureRequest(CameraDevice.TEMPLATE_PREVIEW)
                        req.addTarget(r.surface)
                        req.set(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE_CONTINUOUS_PICTURE)
                        req.set(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_MODE_ON)
                        s.setRepeatingRequest(req.build(), null, camHandler)
                        ui.post { begin() }
                    } catch (e: Exception) {
                        ui.post { say("The camera could not start.", true); finish() }
                    }
                }

                override fun onConfigureFailed(s: CameraCaptureSession) {
                    ui.post { say("The camera could not start.", true); finish() }
                }
            }, camHandler)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            say("The camera could not start.", true)
            finish()
        }
    }

    private fun begin() {
        if (running) return
        running = true
        if (mode == "scene") {
            targetLongSide = 1024
            jpegQuality = 80
            setStatus("Scene description. Hold the phone steady. Double tap to describe again.")
            say("Camera ready. Hold the phone steady.", true)
        } else {
            targetLongSide = 640
            jpegQuality = 65
            setStatus("Live object detection is on. Double tap to pause or continue. Use back to close.")
            say("Live object detection on.", true)
        }
        worker.execute { if (mode == "scene") sceneLoop() else objectLoop() }
    }

    // ---------------- Frames ----------------

    private fun onImage(rd: ImageReader) {
        var img: Image? = null
        try {
            img = rd.acquireLatestImage() ?: return
            if (!wantFrame.getAndSet(false)) return
            val f = makeFrame(img)
            if (f != null) {
                frames.clear()
                frames.offer(f)
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // skip this frame
        } finally {
            try {
                img?.close()
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                // ignore
            }
        }
    }

    private fun makeFrame(img: Image): Frame? {
        val w = img.width
        val h = img.height
        val planes = img.planes
        if (planes.size < 3) return null
        val yBuf = planes[0].buffer
        val uBuf = planes[1].buffer
        val vBuf = planes[2].buffer
        val yRow = planes[0].rowStride
        val uRow = planes[1].rowStride
        val uPix = planes[1].pixelStride
        val vRow = planes[2].rowStride
        val vPix = planes[2].pixelStride
        val nv21 = ByteArray(w * h * 3 / 2)
        var pos = 0
        for (row in 0 until h) {
            yBuf.position(row * yRow)
            yBuf.get(nv21, pos, w)
            pos += w
        }
        val cw = w / 2
        val ch = h / 2
        for (row in 0 until ch) {
            for (col in 0 until cw) {
                nv21[pos++] = vBuf.get(row * vRow + col * vPix)
                nv21[pos++] = uBuf.get(row * uRow + col * uPix)
            }
        }
        val bos = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        YuvImage(nv21, ImageFormat.NV21, w, h, null).compressToJpeg(Rect(0, 0, w, h), 90, bos)
        val raw = bos.toByteArray()

        // Shrink while decoding, then turn the picture upright and scale it to the size we send.
        val longest = maxOf(w, h)
        var sample = 1
        while (longest / (sample * 2) >= targetLongSide) sample *= 2
        val opts = BitmapFactory.Options()
        opts.inSampleSize = sample
        val decoded = BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(raw, 0, raw.size, opts) ?: return null
        val m = Matrix()
        m.postRotate(rotationDegrees().toFloat())
        val scale = targetLongSide.toFloat() / maxOf(decoded.width, decoded.height)
        if (scale < 1f) m.postScale(scale, scale)
        val upright = Bitmap.createBitmap(decoded, 0, 0, decoded.width, decoded.height, m, true)
        if (upright !== decoded) decoded.recycle()

        val out = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        upright.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, jpegQuality, out)

        // A tiny 8x8 grey copy: used to notice whether the view changed at all.
        val tiny = Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(upright, 8, 8, true)
        val sig = IntArray(64)
        for (i in 0 until 64) {
            val p = tiny.getPixel(i % 8, i / 8)
            sig[i] = ((p shr 16 and 0xFF) * 3 + (p shr 8 and 0xFF) * 6 + (p and 0xFF)) / 10
        }
        tiny.recycle()
        upright.recycle()
        return Frame(Base64.encodeToString(out.toByteArray(), Base64.NO_WRAP), sig)
    }

    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
    private fun rotationDegrees(): Int {
        val rot = when (windowManager.defaultDisplay.rotation) {
            Surface.ROTATION_90 -> 90
            Surface.ROTATION_180 -> 180
            Surface.ROTATION_270 -> 270
            else -> 0
        }
        return (sensorOrientation - rot + 360) % 360
    }

    private fun grabFrame(): Frame? {
        frames.clear()
        wantFrame.set(true)
        val f = frames.poll(2500, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)
        wantFrame.set(false)
        return f
    }

    private fun differs(a: IntArray, b: IntArray?): Boolean {
        if (b == null) return true
        var sum = 0
        for (i in a.indices) sum += Math.abs(a[i] - b[i])
        return sum / a.size > 7
    }

    private fun pause(ms: Long) {
        try {
            Thread.sleep(ms)
        } catch (e: InterruptedException) {
            running = false
        }
    }

    // ---------------- Loops ----------------

    private val objectPrompt =
        "You are the live eyes of a blind person who is holding a phone camera. " +
            "Name the main objects and people directly in view, nearest and most important first, " +
            "each with a rough position (left, right, ahead, close, far). " +
            "Reply with ONE very short phrase of at most 12 words. No full sentences, no filler such as " +
            "'I see' or 'the image shows', no markdown. Mention obstacles, stairs, doors, vehicles and people first. "

    private fun objectLoop() {
        val prefs = getSharedPreferences("screen_reader_prefs", MODE_PRIVATE)
        val interval = prefs.getLong("live_interval_ms", 1800L).coerceIn(700L, 10000L)
        pause(1200) // let focus and brightness settle
        while (running) {
            if (paused) {
                pause(250)
                continue
            }
            val t0 = System.currentTimeMillis()
            val f = grabFrame()
            if (f == null) {
                pause(300)
                continue
            }
            // Same view as the last report: save time and quota, say nothing.
            if (!differs(f.sig, lastSig) && lastReport.isNotEmpty()) {
                pause(300)
                continue
            }
            val prompt = objectPrompt +
                (if (lastReport.isNotEmpty()) "Previous report: \"" + lastReport + "\". " +
                    "If nothing important has changed since the previous report, reply with exactly the one word SAME. " else "") +
                "If the picture is too dark or blurry to tell anything, reply with exactly the one word UNCLEAR."
            val r = GeminiClient.describeImage(this, prompt, f.b64, true, 60)
            if (!running) break
            if (r.ok) {
                lastSig = f.sig
                val t = r.text.trim()
                val up = t.uppercase()
                if (up == "SAME" || up.startsWith("SAME.")) {
                    // nothing new
                } else if (up == "UNCLEAR" || up.startsWith("UNCLEAR.")) {
                    val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
                    if (now - lastUnclearAt > 8000) {
                        lastUnclearAt = now
                        say("View unclear", true)
                    }
                } else {
                    lastReport = t
                    say(t, true)
                }
            } else if (r.fatal) {
                ui.post { say(r.text, true) }
                running = false
                ui.post { finish() }
                break
            } else if (r.busy) {
                val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
                if (now - lastSlowAt > 20000) {
                    lastSlowAt = now
                    say("Too many requests. Slowing down.", true)
                }
                pause(6000)
                continue
            } else {
                pause(1500)
                continue
            }
            val spent = System.currentTimeMillis() - t0
            if (spent < interval) pause(interval - spent)
        }
    }

    private val scenePrompt =
        "You are describing what is in front of a blind person who is holding a phone camera. " +
            "In clear plain sentences that sound natural when read aloud, say: what kind of place this is " +
            "(indoors or outdoors), the main objects and people with their positions (left, right, ahead, near, far), " +
            "what people are doing, important colors and the lighting, any readable text (read it out), " +
            "and anything that could be an obstacle or a danger. Start with the most important thing. " +
            "Do not use markdown, lists or asterisks. If the picture is blurry or dark, say so and describe what you can."

    private fun sceneLoop() {
        pause(1800) // give the camera time to focus
        var first = true
        while (running) {
            if (!first && !describeAgain) {
                pause(250)
                continue
            }
            first = false
            describeAgain = false
            ui.post { say("Describing. Please wait.", true) }
            val f = grabFrame()
            if (f == null) {
                ui.post { say("No picture from the camera. Please try again.", true) }
                continue
            }
            val r = GeminiClient.describeImage(this, scenePrompt, f.b64, false, 700)
            if (!running) break
            if (r.ok) {
                MyScreenReaderService.instance?.speakExternalLong(r.text)
            } else {
                ui.post { say(r.text, true) }
                if (r.fatal) {
                    running = false
                    ui.post { finish() }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // ---------------- Closing ----------------

    private fun shutdown() {
        running = false
        wantFrame.set(false)
        try {
            session?.close()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // ignore
        }
        session = null
        try {
            camera?.close()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // ignore
        }
        camera = null
        try {
            reader?.close()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // ignore
        }
        reader = null
    }

    override fun onPause() {
        super.onPause()
        // While the permission question is on the screen the activity is also paused: keep it then.
        if (checkSelfPermission(Manifest.permission.CAMERA) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
            shutdown()
            if (!isFinishing) finish()
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        shutdown()
        try {
            camThread.quitSafely()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // ignore
        }
        worker.shutdownNow()
        super.onDestroy()
    }
}

@@@FILE app/src/main/java/com/mohit/screenreader/GestureConfig.kt lines=145
package com.mohit.screenreader

import android.content.SharedPreferences

// The list of gestures that can be customised, the functions they can run, and the defaults.
object GestureConfig {

    val gestures: List<Pair<String, String>> = listOf(
        "swipe_right" to "Swipe right",
        "swipe_left" to "Swipe left",
        "swipe_up" to "Swipe up",

    // Android's numeric ids for the multi finger gestures (same numbers TalkBack uses).

        29 to "f3_up", 30 to "f3_down", 31 to "f3_left", 32 to "f3_right",
        37 to "f4_tap1", 38 to "f4_tap2", 39 to "f4_tap3", 42 to "f4_tap2_hold",
        33 to "f4_up", 34 to "f4_down", 35 to "f4_left", 36 to "f4_right"
    )

    val functions: List<Pair<String, String>> = listOf(
        "none" to "Do nothing",
        "next" to "Next item",
        "previous" to "Previous item",
        "activate" to "Activate current item",
        "read_next" to "Next part of the item (per reading control)",
        "read_prev" to "Previous part of the item (per reading control)",
        "control_next" to "Next reading control",
        "control_prev" to "Previous reading control",
        "menu" to "Screen reader menu",
        "back" to "Back",
        "home" to "Home",
        "recents" to "Recent apps",
        "notifications" to "Notifications",
        "quick_settings" to "Quick settings",
        "power_dialog" to "Power menu",
        "lock_screen" to "Lock screen",
        "screenshot" to "Take screenshot",
        "copy" to "Copy focused item",
        "append" to "Append focused item to copied text",
        "cut" to "Cut",
        "paste" to "Paste",
        "clear_edit" to "Clear edit box",
        "describe_screen" to "AI: Describe screen",
        "describe_image" to "AI: Describe image",
        "scene_camera" to "AI: Scene description with camera",
        "object_camera" to "AI: Live object detection with camera",
        "voice_typing" to "AI: Voice typing",
        "translate" to "Translate (popup)",
        "voice_assistant" to "Voice assistant (offline commands)",
        "hold_drag" to "Touch and hold, then drag (voice message record)",
        "scroll_forward" to "Scroll list forward",
        "scroll_backward" to "Scroll list backward",
        "repeat" to "Repeat last spoken text",
        "stop_speech" to "Stop speech",
        "volume_up" to "Speech volume up",
        "volume_down" to "Speech volume down"
    )

    val defaults: Map<String, String> = mapOf(
        "swipe_right" to "next",
        "swipe_left" to "previous",
        "swipe_down" to "read_next",
        "swipe_up" to "read_prev",
        "down_up" to "control_next",
        "up_down" to "control_prev",
        "down_right" to "menu",
        "down_left" to "back",
        "up_left" to "home",
        "left_up" to "recents",
        "right_down" to "notifications",
        "up_right" to "quick_settings",
        "double_tap_hold" to "hold_drag"
    )

    fun functionFor(prefs: SharedPreferences, gestureKey: String): String {
        return prefs.getString("gesture_" + gestureKey, null) ?: defaults[gestureKey] ?: "none"
    }

    // Fixed functions plus every extension and auto click function that exists right now.
    fun allFunctions(prefs: SharedPreferences): List<Pair<String, String>> {
        val list = mutableListOf<Pair<String, String>>()
        list.addAll(functions)
        for (e in Extensions.builtin) list.add(("ext:" + e.first) to ("Extension: " + e.second + " (turn on or off)"))
        for (m in AutoClickStore.load(prefs)) list.add(("macro:" + m.name) to ("Auto click: " + m.name))
        return list
    }

    fun functionLabel(id: String): String {
        functions.firstOrNull { it.first == id }?.let { return it.second }
        if (id.startsWith("ext:")) {
            val eid = id.substring(4)
            return "Extension: " + (Extensions.builtin.firstOrNull { it.first == eid }?.second ?: eid)
        }
        if (id.startsWith("macro:")) return "Auto click: " + id.substring(6)
        return id
    }
}
@@@FILE app/src/main/java/com/mohit/screenreader/GestureSettingsActivity.kt lines=77
package com.mohit.screenreader

import android.app.Activity
import android.app.AlertDialog
import android.content.Context
import android.os.Bundle
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.widget.ArrayAdapter
import android.widget.Button
import android.widget.ListView

class GestureSettingsActivity : Activity() {

    private lateinit var list: ListView
    private lateinit var adapter: ArrayAdapter<String>
    private val rows = mutableListOf<String>()

    private fun prefs() = getSharedPreferences("screen_reader_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_gesture_settings)
        list = findViewById(R.id.listGestures)
        adapter = ArrayAdapter(this, android.R.layout.simple_list_item_1, rows)
        list.adapter = adapter
        refreshRows()

        list.setOnItemClickListener { _, _, position, _ -> chooseFunction(position) }
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnResetGestures).setOnClickListener {
            val e = prefs().edit()
            for (g in GestureConfig.gestures) e.remove("gesture_" + g.first)
            e.apply()
            refreshRows()
            tell("All gestures were reset to their defaults.")
        }
    }

    private fun refreshRows() {
        rows.clear()
        for (g in GestureConfig.gestures) {
            rows.add(g.second + ": " + GestureConfig.functionLabel(GestureConfig.functionFor(prefs(), g.first)))
        }
        adapter.notifyDataSetChanged()
    }

    // Announce a little later so it is not cut off by the screen reader reading the screen again.
    private fun tell(message: String) {
        Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed({ list.announceForAccessibility(message) }, 900)
    }

    private fun chooseFunction(position: Int) {
        val gesture = GestureConfig.gestures[position]
        val current = GestureConfig.functionFor(prefs(), gesture.first)
        val funcs = GestureConfig.allFunctions(prefs())
        val labels = funcs.map { it.second }.toTypedArray()
        AlertDialog.Builder(this)
            .setTitle(gesture.second + ". Choose what it does")
            .setItems(labels) { _, which ->
                val chosen = funcs[which].first
                if (current == "menu" && chosen != "menu" && !menuMappedElsewhere(gesture.first)) {
                    tell("Not changed. Give the Screen reader menu to another gesture first, otherwise you could not open it.")
                } else {
                    prefs().edit().putString("gesture_" + gesture.first, chosen).apply()
                    refreshRows()
                    tell(gesture.second + " now does: " + GestureConfig.functionLabel(chosen))
                }
            }
            .show()
    }

    private fun menuMappedElsewhere(exceptKey: String): Boolean {
        return GestureConfig.gestures.any {
            it.first != exceptKey && GestureConfig.functionFor(prefs(), it.first) == "menu"
        }
    }
}

            startActivity(Intent(Settings.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS))
        }

        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnGestures).setOnClickListener {
            startActivity(Intent(this, GestureSettingsActivity::class.java))
        }

        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnCustomizeMenu).setOnClickListener {
            val i = Intent(this, CustomizeListActivity::class.java)
            i.putExtra("kind", "menu")
            startActivity(i)
        }

        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnCustomizeReading).setOnClickListener {
            val i = Intent(this, CustomizeListActivity::class.java)
            i.putExtra("kind", "reading")
            startActivity(i)
        }

        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnExtensions).setOnClickListener {
            startActivity(Intent(this, ExtensionsActivity::class.java))
        }

        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnGaming).setOnClickListener {
            startActivity(Intent(this, GamingAppsActivity::class.java))
        }

        val proxButton = findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnProximity)
        val prefs = getSharedPreferences("screen_reader_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        fun showProximity() {
            val on = prefs.getBoolean("proximity_silence", true)
            proxButton.text = "Proximity sensor silences speech: " + (if (on) "On" else "Off")
        }
        showProximity()
        proxButton.setOnClickListener {
            prefs.edit().putBoolean("proximity_silence", !prefs.getBoolean("proximity_silence", true)).apply()
            showProximity()
            proxButton.announceForAccessibility(proxButton.text.toString())
        }

        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnApiKey).setOnClickListener {
            startActivity(Intent(this, ApiKeyActivity::class.java))
        }
    }
}

package com.mohit.screenreader

import android.Manifest
import android.app.Activity
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.os.Bundle

// Opened by the screen reader service when voice typing needs the microphone permission.
class MicPermissionActivity : Activity() {

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        if (checkSelfPermission(Manifest.permission.RECORD_AUDIO) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
            finish()
            return
        }
        requestPermissions(arrayOf(Manifest.permission.RECORD_AUDIO), 1)
    }

    override fun onRequestPermissionsResult(requestCode: Int, permissions: Array<out String>, grantResults: IntArray) {
        super.onRequestPermissionsResult(requestCode, permissions, grantResults)
        finish()
    }
}

package com.mohit.screenreader

import android.accessibilityservice.AccessibilityService

/**
 * MyScreenReaderService - first working version (v1).
 *
 * Standard screen-reader interface:
 *   - swipe right  -> next item, spoken aloud
 *   - swipe left   -> previous item, spoken aloud
 *   - double tap   -> activate (click) the current item
 *
 * A short sound plays for every action too (from the selected sound
 * scheme), and if speech ever fails to fire, an alert sound plays instead
 * of staying silent, so a failure is never invisible.
 *
 * IMPORTANT while testing: TalkBack should be switched OFF before turning
 * this service on. Two services both asking for touch-exploration/gesture
 * handling at once is exactly the conflict noted while building the
 * cricket game - only one of them should own gestures at a time.
 */
class MyScreenReaderService : AccessibilityService() {

    private var tts: TextToSpeech? = null
    private var soundPool: SoundPool? = null

    private var soundFocus: Int? = null
    private var soundActivate: Int? = null
    private var soundScreenChange: Int? = null
    private var soundAlert: Int? = null
    private var soundFocusActionable: Int? = null
    private val schemeSoundIds = mutableListOf<Int>()
    private val loadedSoundIds = mutableSetOf<Int>()

    private val nodeList = mutableListOf<AccessibilityNodeInfo>()
    private var currentIndex = -1
    private var focusedNode: AccessibilityNodeInfo? = null

    private val schemeAssetFolder = "sound_iphone_voiceover"

    private val handler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private var busyScrolling = false
    private var lastSpoken = ""

    // ---- AI (Gemini) ----
    private val aiExecutor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()
    private var aiBusy = false
    private val aiLabels = listOf("Describe screen", "Describe image", "Voice typing", "Scene description with camera", "Live object detection with camera", "Back")
    private var aiIndex = 0

    // ---- Auto focus on new screens ----
    private var autoFocusPending: Runnable? = null
    private var lastAutoFocusAt = 0L

    // ---- Gaming mode ----
    private var gamingActive = false
    private var lastForegroundPackage: String? = null
    private var gamingSuppressedPackage: String? = null
    private var volUpHeld = false
    private var volDownHeld = false
    private var lastGameText = ""
    private var lastGameTextAt = 0L

    // ---- Slider value feedback ----
    private var sliderSpoken = false
    private var sliderWatchUntil = 0L

    // ---- Screen Reader Menu state (opened with swipe down-then-right) ----
    private enum class Mode { NORMAL, MENU, UNIT_CHOOSER, ITEM_SELECT, AI_MENU, EXT_MENU, VOICE_MENU }
    private enum class SplitKind { CHAR, WORD, LINE, PARAGRAPH }

    private var mode = Mode.NORMAL
    private var menuIndex = 0
    private var menuTarget: AccessibilityNodeInfo? = null
    private val clipList = mutableListOf<String>()
    private var suppressWindowEventsUntil = 0L

    // The menu is built from the items switched on in "Customize screen reader menu".
    private var menuIds: List<String> = listOf("close")
    private val menuLabels: List<String>
        get() = menuIds.map { id ->
            if (id == "close") "Close menu" else (MenuConfig.menuItems.firstOrNull { it.first == id }?.second ?: id)
        }

    private fun refreshMenuIds() {
        val off = MenuConfig.disabled(getSharedPreferences("screen_reader_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE), "menu")
        menuIds = MenuConfig.menuItems.map { it.first }.filter { !off.contains(it) } + "close"
    }

    // ---- Extensions menu ----
    private var extIds: List<String> = emptyList()
    private var extIndex = 0
    private var recognizer: SpeechRecognizer? = null
    private var assistantActive = false
    private var proximityListener: SensorEventListener? = null
    private var assistantHearing = false
    private var assistantTouchOkAfter = Long.MAX_VALUE
    @Volatile private var voiceLastSpeechAt = 0L
    private var voiceTouchOkAfter = Long.MAX_VALUE

    // ---- AI voice typing ----
    private val voiceLanguages = listOf(
        "Hindi" to "hi", "English" to "en", "Urdu" to "ur", "Punjabi" to "pa", "Marathi" to "mr",
        "Gujarati" to "gu", "Bengali" to "bn", "Tamil" to "ta", "Telugu" to "te", "Kannada" to "kn",
        "Malayalam" to "ml", "Odia" to "or", "Assamese" to "as", "Nepali" to "ne",
        "Spanish" to "es", "French" to "fr", "Arabic" to "ar", "German" to "de"
    )
    private var voiceLabels: List<String> = emptyList()
    private var voiceIds: List<String> = emptyList()
    private var voiceIndex = 0
    private var voiceActive = false
    @Volatile private var voiceStopRequested = false
    private var voiceCaptureDone = true
    private val voicePending = AtomicInteger(0)
    private val voiceTyped = StringBuilder()
    private var voiceLastError: String? = null
    private val voiceExecutor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()
    private var macroRunning = false
    private val youtubePackage = "com.google.android.youtube"

    private val unitLabels = listOf("character", "word", "line", "paragraph")
    private var unitIndex = 0
    private var splitKind = SplitKind.WORD
    private var selectUnits: List<String> = emptyList()
    private var selectedFlags = BooleanArray(0)
    private var selectIndex = 0

    // ---- Reading controls: change with swipe down-then-up / up-then-down,
    // ---- then move inside the focused item with plain swipe down / up ----
    private val readingLabels = listOf("Normal item", "Character", "Word", "Line", "Paragraph", "Actions", "Slider", "Heading", "Link", "Edit box")
    private var readingIndex = 0
    private var readUnits: List<String> = emptyList()
    private var readIndex = -1
    private var lastModalHoverHintAt = 0L

    // ---- Actions control + edit-box selection mode ----
    private val actionsControlIndex = 5
    private val sliderControlIndex = 6
    private val headingControlIndex = 7
    private val linkControlIndex = 8
    private val editBoxControlIndex = 9
    private var actionIndex = -1
    private var selecting = false
    private var selAnchor = 0
    private var selCursor = 0

    // ---- Accessibility volume / TTS settings ----
    private var touching = false
    private val accessibilityStream = 10 // AudioManager.STREAM_ACCESSIBILITY
    private var ttsRate = 1.0f
    private var ttsVolume = 1.0f
    private var prefsListener: SharedPreferences.OnSharedPreferenceChangeListener? = null

    override fun onServiceConnected() {
        super.onServiceConnected()
        instance = this
        startProximity()

        val prefs = getSharedPreferences("screen_reader_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val enginePackage = prefs.getString("tts_engine_package", null)

        handler.removeCallbacks(skipLoop)
        handler.postDelayed(skipLoop, 2000)

        ttsRate = prefs.getFloat("tts_rate", 1.0f)
        ttsVolume = prefs.getFloat("tts_volume", 1.0f)
        val changeListener = SharedPreferences.OnSharedPreferenceChangeListener { p, key ->
            if (key == "tts_rate") {
                ttsRate = p.getFloat("tts_rate", 1.0f)
                tts?.setSpeechRate(ttsRate)
            } else if (key == "tts_volume") {
                ttsVolume = p.getFloat("tts_volume", 1.0f)
            } else if (key == "sound_scheme") {
                handler.post { loadSchemeSounds() }
            }
        }
        prefsListener = changeListener
        prefs.registerOnSharedPreferenceChangeListener(changeListener)

        // Ask Android for a separate "accessibility volume" channel
        // (0x80 = FLAG_ENABLE_ACCESSIBILITY_VOLUME) so speech is not tied to media volume.
        try {
            val info = serviceInfo
            if (info != null) {
                info.flags = info.flags or 0x00000080
                serviceInfo = info
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // Not fatal: speech still works, just possibly on the shared volume.
        }

        val initListener = TextToSpeech.OnInitListener { status ->
            if (status == TextToSpeech.SUCCESS) {
                // Speak on the accessibility audio channel instead of the media channel.
                tts?.setAudioAttributes(
                    AudioAttributes.Builder()
                        .setUsage(AudioAttributes.USAGE_ASSISTANCE_ACCESSIBILITY)
                        .setContentType(AudioAttributes.CONTENT_TYPE_SPEECH)
                        .build()
                )
                tts?.setSpeechRate(ttsRate)
                tts?.setOnUtteranceProgressListener(object : UtteranceProgressListener() {
                    override fun onStart(utteranceId: String?) {}
                    override fun onDone(utteranceId: String?) {}
                    override fun onError(utteranceId: String?) {
                        playSound(soundAlert)
                    }
                })
            } else {
                playSound(soundAlert)
            }
        }

        tts = if (enginePackage != null) {
            TextToSpeech(this, initListener, enginePackage)
        } else {
            TextToSpeech(this, initListener)
        }

        val attrs = AudioAttributes.Builder()
            .setUsage(AudioAttributes.USAGE_ASSISTANCE_ACCESSIBILITY)
            .setContentType(AudioAttributes.CONTENT_TYPE_SONIFICATION)
            .build()
        soundPool = SoundPool.Builder()
            .setMaxStreams(4)
            .setAudioAttributes(attrs)
            .build()
        soundPool?.setOnLoadCompleteListener { _, sampleId, status ->
            if (status == 0) {
                loadedSoundIds.add(sampleId)
            }
        }

        loadSchemeSounds()
    }

    // Loads the four cues (and the "button" focus cue) of the sound scheme chosen in the app.
    private fun loadSchemeSounds() {
        val pool = soundPool ?: return
        for (id in schemeSoundIds) {
            try {
                pool.unload(id)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                // ignore
            }
        }
        schemeSoundIds.clear()
        loadedSoundIds.clear()
        soundFocusActionable = null
        val scheme = getSharedPreferences("screen_reader_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .getString("sound_scheme", "iphone_voiceover")
        if (scheme == "talkback") {
            val dir = TalkbackTheme.dir(this)
            soundFocus = loadFileSound(dir, "focus.ogg")
            soundActivate = loadFileSound(dir, "tick.ogg")
            soundScreenChange = loadFileSound(dir, "window_state.ogg")
            soundAlert = loadFileSound(dir, "double_beep.ogg")
            soundFocusActionable = loadFileSound(dir, "focus_actionable.ogg")
        } else {
            soundFocus = loadSound("focus2.ogg")
            soundActivate = loadSound("click.ogg")
            soundScreenChange = loadSound("ScreenChange.ogg")
            soundAlert = loadSound("Alert.ogg")
        }
    }

    private fun loadFileSound(dir: java.io.File?, name: String): Int? {
        return try {
            val f = java.io.File(dir ?: return null, name)
            if (!f.exists()) return null
            val id = soundPool?.load(f.absolutePath, 1)
            if (id != null) schemeSoundIds.add(id)
            id
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }

    private fun loadSound(filename: String): Int? {
        return try {
            val afd = assets.openFd("$schemeAssetFolder/$filename")
            val id = soundPool?.load(afd, 1)
            afd.close()
            if (id != null) schemeSoundIds.add(id)
            id
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // Sound scheme file not bundled in this build yet - stays silent
            // for this one cue rather than crashing the service.
            null
        }
    }

    private fun playSound(soundId: Int?) {
        if (soundId != null && loadedSoundIds.contains(soundId)) {
            soundPool?.play(soundId, 1f, 1f, 1, 0, 1f)
        }
    }

    companion object {
        @Volatile
        var instance: MyScreenReaderService? = null
    }

    // Used by the camera screen to speak what the AI sees.
    fun speakExternal(text: String, flush: Boolean) {
        handler.post {
            val engine = tts ?: return@post
            lastSpoken = text
            val params = Bundle()
            params.putFloat(TextToSpeech.Engine.KEY_PARAM_VOLUME, ttsVolume)
            engine.speak(text, if (flush) TextToSpeech.QUEUE_FLUSH else TextToSpeech.QUEUE_ADD, params, "x" + System.currentTimeMillis())
        }
    }

    fun speakExternalLong(text: String) {
        handler.post { speakLong(text) }
    }

    private fun speak(text: String) {
        val engine = tts
        if (engine == null) {
            playSound(soundAlert)
            return
        }
        lastSpoken = text
        val utteranceId = "u" + System.currentTimeMillis()
        val params = Bundle()
        params.putFloat(TextToSpeech.Engine.KEY_PARAM_VOLUME, ttsVolume)
        val result = engine.speak(text, TextToSpeech.QUEUE_FLUSH, params, utteranceId)
        if (result == TextToSpeech.ERROR) {
            playSound(soundAlert)
        }
    }

    override fun onAccessibilityEvent(event: AccessibilityEvent?) {
        event ?: return
        when (event.eventType) {
            AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_WINDOW_STATE_CHANGED -> {
                // The on-screen keyboard is its own window - it must not reset our focus.
                if (isInputMethodEvent(event)) return
                updateGamingMode(event.packageName?.toString())
                // While the menu is open, or right after we touch the clipboard
                // (Android 13 shows a system "copied" popup), ignore this so the
                // menu / focus is not reset.
                if (mode != Mode.NORMAL || System.currentTimeMillis() < suppressWindowEventsUntil) return
                if (gamingActive) return
                scheduleAutoFocus(event.text.joinToString(" ").trim())
            }
            AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_WINDOW_CONTENT_CHANGED -> {
                if (gamingActive) speakGameText(event)
            }
            AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_VIEW_TEXT_CHANGED -> {
                if (mode == Mode.NORMAL && !gamingActive &&
                    System.currentTimeMillis() >= suppressWindowEventsUntil) echoTyping(event)
            }
            AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_ANNOUNCEMENT -> {
                // Apps (like YouTube) announce things such as a new slider position this way.
                if (mode != Mode.NORMAL) return
                val said = event.text.joinToString(" ").trim()
                if (said.isNotEmpty()) {
                    if (System.currentTimeMillis() < sliderWatchUntil) sliderSpoken = true
                    speak(said)
                }
            }
            AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_TOUCH_INTERACTION_START -> {
                touching = true
                if (voiceActive || assistantActive) stopByTouchIfAllowed()
            }
            AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_TOUCH_INTERACTION_END -> {
                touching = false
            }
            AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_VIEW_HOVER_ENTER -> {
                if (mode != Mode.NORMAL) {
                    hintModalEntry()
                    return
                }
                handleHover(event)
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Explore-by-touch: when a finger is dragged (not swiped) directly onto a
     * screen item, Android's touch-exploration layer (enabled via
     * flagRequestTouchExplorationMode in accessibility_service_config.xml)
     * automatically hit-tests and reports the exact view under the finger as
     * a HOVER_ENTER event - no manual coordinate math needed here. This is
     * on top of (not instead of) swipe navigation and double-tap activation.
     */
    private fun handleHover(event: AccessibilityEvent) {
        val raw = event.source ?: return
        // If the finger is on a piece of text inside a clickable row, treat the
        // whole row as the item (same as swipe navigation does).
        var node: AccessibilityNodeInfo = raw
        if (!node.isClickable && !isSlider(node)) {
            var p = node.parent
            var d = 0
            while (p != null && d < 10) {
                if ((d < 4 && p.isClickable && labelOf(p) != null) || tbGroupText(p) != null) {
                    node = p
                    break
                }
                p = p.parent
                d++
            }
        }
        if (!node.isVisibleToUser) return
        if (!isSlider(node) && labelOf(node) == null && !tbShouldFocus(node)) return

        focusedNode = node
        syncNativeTouch(node)
        resetFocusState()
        // Best-effort: keep swipe navigation picking up from near where the
        // finger just was.
        val hovered = node
        val matchIndex = nodeList.indexOfFirst { it == hovered }
        if (matchIndex >= 0) currentIndex = matchIndex

        node.performAction(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_FOCUS)
        playSound(soundFocus)
        speak(spokenLabel(node))
    }

    private fun rebuildNodeList() {
        nodeList.clear()
        val root = rootInActiveWindow
        if (root != null) collectNodes(root, nodeList, false)
        // The on-screen keyboard and floating system controls (for example the screen
        // recorder's stop button) are separate windows: include them after the app's items.
        try {
            val activeId = root?.windowId ?: -1
            val dm = resources.displayMetrics
            val thinLimit = (100 * dm.density).toInt()
            val bounds = Rect()
            val extra = windows.filter { it.id != activeId }.sortedBy { w ->
                if (w.type == AccessibilityWindowInfo.TYPE_INPUT_METHOD) 0 else 1
            }
            for (w in extra) {
                val t = w.type
                val sameApp = t == AccessibilityWindowInfo.TYPE_APPLICATION &&
                    root?.packageName != null && w.root?.packageName == root.packageName
                if (t != AccessibilityWindowInfo.TYPE_INPUT_METHOD &&
                    t != AccessibilityWindowInfo.TYPE_SYSTEM &&
                    t != AccessibilityWindowInfo.TYPE_ACCESSIBILITY_OVERLAY && !sameApp) continue
                val r = w.root ?: continue
                if (r.packageName?.toString() == packageName) continue
                if (t != AccessibilityWindowInfo.TYPE_INPUT_METHOD) {
                    // Skip the full-width status bar and navigation bar strips.
                    w.getBoundsInScreen(bounds)
                    if (bounds.width() >= dm.widthPixels * 9 / 10 && bounds.height() < thinLimit) continue
                }
                collectNodes(r, nodeList, false)
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // Extra windows are optional.
        }
        dedupeNeighbors(nodeList)
    }

    // ---- Labels: one item per row, never an empty "item" followed by its own text ----

    private fun tipOf(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo): String? {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 28) return null
        return node.tooltipText?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
    }

    private fun ownLabel(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo): String? {
        return node.text?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
            ?: node.contentDescription?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
            ?: tipOf(node)
    }

    // Text of the non-clickable descendants of a clickable row, joined together.
    private fun gatherText(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo, out: MutableList<String>, depth: Int) {
        if (depth > 6 || out.size > 8) return
        for (i in 0 until node.childCount) {
            val c = node.getChild(i) ?: continue
            if (!c.isVisibleToUser) continue
            if (clickableNode(c)) continue // a separate control, it is its own item
            val own = ownLabel(c)
            if (own != null) out.add(own) else gatherText(c, out, depth + 1)
        }
    }

    private fun mergedText(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo): String? {
        if (!node.isClickable || node.isScrollable) return null
        val parts = mutableListOf<String>()
        gatherText(node, parts, 0)
        if (parts.isEmpty() || parts.size > 8) return null
        return parts.joinToString(", ")
    }

    // Full spoken label: its own text plus the text of plain children (e.g. a quick-settings tile
    // whose name and "On/Off" are separate views) plus its state (On/Off, checked).
    private fun labelOf(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo): String? {
        tbGroupText(node)?.let { return it }
        val parts = mutableListOf<String>()
        val own = ownLabel(node)
        if (own != null) parts.add(own)
        if (node.isClickable) {
            // Tiles often keep the name in the description and only the extra info in the text.
            val cd = node.contentDescription?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
            if (cd != null && parts.none { it.contains(cd, true) || cd.contains(it, true) }) parts.add(0, cd)
            try {
                val lb = node.labeledBy?.let { ownLabel(it) }
                if (lb != null && parts.none { it.contains(lb, true) }) parts.add(0, lb)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                // optional
            }
        }
        if ((clickableNode(node) || tbActionable(node)) && !node.isScrollable && (own == null || own.length <= 40)) {
            val g = mutableListOf<String>()
            gatherText(node, g, 0)
            if (g.size <= 8) {
                for (x in g) if (parts.none { it.contains(x, true) }) parts.add(x)
            }
        }
        if (parts.isEmpty()) return null
        if (!isSlider(node)) {
            var state: String? = null
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 30) {
                state = node.stateDescription?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
            }
            if (state == null && node.isCheckable) {
                val cls = node.className?.toString() ?: ""
                state = if (cls.contains("Switch") || cls.contains("Toggle")) {
                    if (node.isChecked) "On" else "Off"
                } else {
                    if (node.isChecked) "checked" else "not checked"
                }
            }
            val st = state
            if (st != null && parts.none { it.contains(st, true) }) parts.add(st)
        }
        return parts.joinToString(", ")
    }

    // ---- Which items get focus: the same rules Google TalkBack uses ----
    // An item gets focus only if it is visible and (a) it can be clicked / focused and has
    // something to say, or (b) it has text and no ancestor already takes the focus.
    // Containers with nothing to say never get focus, so there is no empty "item" any more.

    private fun tbHasText(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        if (n.collectionInfo != null) return false
        return !n.text.isNullOrEmpty() || !n.contentDescription.isNullOrEmpty() || !n.hintText.isNullOrEmpty() || tipOf(n) != null
    }

    private fun tbHasState(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 30 && !n.stateDescription.isNullOrEmpty()) return true
        if (n.isCheckable) return true
        val r = n.rangeInfo
        return r != null && r.max > r.min
    }

    // Can the user press this? Only real press actions count. Reading actions (move by word,
    // line...) exist on every view that has text, so they must NOT make a plain text "pressable".
    private fun tbActionable(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        if (n.isClickable || n.isLongClickable || n.isFocusable) return true
        return hasClickAction(n)
    }

    private fun hasClickAction(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        for (a in n.actionList) {
            val id = a.id
