            if (id == AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_CLICK || id == AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_LONG_CLICK) return true
        }
        return false
    }

    // Jieshuo also treats a view that only advertises the click action as clickable.
    private fun clickableNode(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean = n.isClickable || hasClickAction(n)

    private fun tbFocusableOrClickable(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        if (!n.isVisibleToUser) return false
        val srf = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 28 && n.isScreenReaderFocusable
        return srf || tbActionable(n)
    }

    private fun tbIsScrollItem(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo?): Boolean {
        if (n == null) return false
        val cls = n.className?.toString() ?: ""
        if (cls.contains("ViewPager")) return false
        return n.isScrollable || n.collectionInfo != null || cls.contains("ListView") ||
            cls.contains("GridView") || cls.contains("RecyclerView")
    }

    private fun tbTopLevelScrollItem(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        return n.isVisibleToUser && tbIsScrollItem(n.parent)
    }

    // Does this node (or a plain, non-clickable descendant) have something to speak?
    private fun tbSpeaking(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo, depth: Int = 0): Boolean {
        if (depth > 12) return false
        if (tbHasText(n) || tbHasState(n)) return true
        for (i in 0 until n.childCount) {
            val c = n.getChild(i) ?: continue
            if (!c.isVisibleToUser) continue
            if (tbFocusableOrClickable(c)) continue // a separate item with its own focus
            if (tbTopLevelScrollItem(c) && tbSpeaking(c, depth + 1) && !n.isClickable && !n.isLongClickable) continue
            if (tbSpeaking(c, depth + 1)) return true
        }
        // Invisible children with text that cannot be acted on still count (TalkBack does the same).
        for (i in 0 until n.childCount) {
            val c = n.getChild(i) ?: continue
            if (!c.isVisibleToUser && tbHasText(c) && !tbActionable(c)) return true
        }
        return false
    }

    private fun tbShouldFocusNoChildren(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        return tbFocusableOrClickable(n) || (tbTopLevelScrollItem(n) && tbSpeaking(n))
    }

    private fun tbShouldFocus(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        if (!n.isVisibleToUser) return false
        // A big container that fills its whole window and does nothing itself is skipped.
        try {
            val w = n.window
            if (w != null && n.childCount > 0 && !tbFocusableOrClickable(n)) {
                val wb = Rect()
                w.getBoundsInScreen(wb)
                if (wb == boundsOf(n)) return false
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // optional
        }
        val focusable = tbShouldFocusNoChildren(n)
        if (!focusable) {
            if (!(tbHasText(n) || tbHasState(n))) return false
            var p: AccessibilityNodeInfo? = n.parent
            var d = 0
            while (p != null && d < 30) {
                if (tbShouldFocusNoChildren(p) && (ownLabel(p) != null || tbSpeaking(p) || tbGroupText(p) != null)) return false
                p = p.parent
                d++
            }
            return true
        }
        if (n.childCount == 0) return true
        if (tbSpeaking(n)) return true
        // A button with nothing to say but no other controls inside: still a real control.
        return !tbHasActionableDescendant(n) && !coversWholeScreen(n)
    }

    // ---- One message row = one item (WhatsApp: "You, Voice message, 20 seconds, 8:30 PM, Delivered") ----

    private fun groupActionable(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        return tbFocusableOrClickable(n) || isSlider(n) || n.rangeInfo != null
    }

    private fun groupParts(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo, out: MutableList<String>, depth: Int) {
        if (depth > 10 || out.size > 12) return
        for (i in 0 until n.childCount) {
            val c = n.getChild(i) ?: continue
            if (!c.isVisibleToUser) continue
            if (groupActionable(c)) continue // a real control stays its own item
            if (tbIsScrollItem(c)) continue
            val own = ownLabel(c)
            if (own != null) out.add(own) else groupParts(c, out, depth + 1)
        }
    }

    // A plain (not clickable) row directly inside a list that holds several pieces of text:
    // they are read together as one item. Buttons and sliders inside it stay separate.
    private fun tbGroupText(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): String? {
        if (n.childCount < 2) return null
        if (tbFocusableOrClickable(n) || tbIsScrollItem(n) || isSlider(n)) return null
        val p = n.parent ?: return null
        if (!tbIsScrollItem(p)) return null
        val parts = mutableListOf<String>()
        groupParts(n, parts, 0)
        if (parts.size < 2) return null
        return parts.joinToString(", ")
    }

    private fun tbHasActionableDescendant(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo, depth: Int = 0): Boolean {
        if (depth > 8) return false
        for (i in 0 until n.childCount) {
            val c = n.getChild(i) ?: continue
            if (!c.isVisibleToUser) continue
            if (tbFocusableOrClickable(c)) return true
            if (tbHasActionableDescendant(c, depth + 1)) return true
        }
        return false
    }

    private fun coversWholeScreen(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        val dm = resources.displayMetrics
        val b = boundsOf(n)
        return b.width() >= dm.widthPixels * 9 / 10 && b.height() >= dm.heightPixels * 8 / 10
    }

    // An item with no text at all is only worth a stop if it is a real control (button, switch,
    // edit box). Empty decoration is skipped, so there are no empty "unlabeled" stops.
    private fun isWorthFocus(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        if (labelOf(n) != null) return true
        return clickableNode(n) || n.isLongClickable || n.isCheckable || isEditBoxNode(n)
    }

    private fun collectNodes(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo?, out: MutableList<AccessibilityNodeInfo>, inGroup: Boolean) {
        if (node == null) return
        if (!node.isVisibleToUser) return
        var groupHere = inGroup
        if (!inGroup && tbGroupText(node) != null) {
            out.add(node)
            groupHere = true
        } else if (inGroup) {
            // Inside a merged row only real controls are separate items.
            if (groupActionable(node) && (isSlider(node) || tbShouldFocus(node))) out.add(node)
        } else if (isSlider(node) || (tbShouldFocus(node) && isWorthFocus(node))) {
            out.add(node)
        }
        for (i in 0 until node.childCount) {
            collectNodes(node.getChild(i), out, groupHere)
        }
    }

    // If a name is already inside the item right next to it (a folder and its own name),
    // keep only the one that says it all.
    private fun dedupeNeighbors(list: MutableList<AccessibilityNodeInfo>) {
        val gap = (16 * resources.displayMetrics.density).toInt()
        var i = 0
        while (i < list.size - 1) {
            val a = list[i]
            val b = list[i + 1]
            val la = labelOf(a)
            val lb = labelOf(b)
            if (la != null && lb != null && !isSlider(a) && !isSlider(b)) {
                val ra = Rect(boundsOf(a)).apply { inset(-gap, -gap) }
                val rb = Rect(boundsOf(b)).apply { inset(-gap, -gap) }
                if (Rect.intersects(ra, rb)) {
                    val na = normLabel(la)
                    val nb = normLabel(lb)
                    if (!tbFocusableOrClickable(b) && na.contains(nb)) {
                        list.removeAt(i + 1)
                        continue
                    }
                    if (!tbFocusableOrClickable(a) && nb.contains(na)) {
                        list.removeAt(i)
                        continue
                    }
                }
            }
            i++
        }
    }

    // ---- Keeping the swipe list fresh (feeds like YouTube load items after the screen opens) ----

    private fun isDescendantOf(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo, ancestor: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        var cur: AccessibilityNodeInfo? = node.parent
        var d = 0
        while (cur != null && d < 20) {
            if (cur == ancestor) return true
            cur = cur.parent
            d++
        }
        return false
    }

    private fun boundsOf(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Rect {
        val r = Rect()
        node.getBoundsInScreen(r)
        return r
    }

    // Rebuilds the list from the live screen and finds the currently focused item in it again.
    private fun syncList(): Boolean {
        val old = focusedNode
        val oldLabel = old?.let { labelOf(it) }
        val oldTop = old?.let { boundsOf(it).top } ?: 0
        rebuildNodeList()
        if (old != null) {
            var idx = nodeList.indexOfFirst { it == old }
            if (idx < 0 && oldLabel != null) {
                var best = -1
                var bestDiff = Int.MAX_VALUE
                nodeList.forEachIndexed { i, n ->
                    if (labelOf(n) == oldLabel) {
                        val diff = Math.abs(boundsOf(n).top - oldTop)
                        if (diff < bestDiff) {
                            bestDiff = diff
                            best = i
                        }
                    }
                }
                idx = best
            }
            if (idx >= 0) {
                currentIndex = idx
                focusedNode = nodeList[idx]
                return true
            }
        }
        currentIndex = if (nodeList.isEmpty()) -1 else minOf(currentIndex, nodeList.size - 1)
        return false
    }

    private fun findScrollableAncestor(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo?): AccessibilityNodeInfo? {
        var cur: AccessibilityNodeInfo? = node
        var d = 0
        while (cur != null && d < 25) {
            if (cur.isScrollable) return cur
            cur = cur.parent
            d++
        }
        return null
    }

    private fun findAnyScrollable(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo?, depth: Int = 0): AccessibilityNodeInfo? {
        if (node == null || depth > 25) return null
        if (node.isVisibleToUser && node.isScrollable) return node
        for (i in 0 until node.childCount) {
            val f = findAnyScrollable(node.getChild(i), depth + 1)
            if (f != null) return f
        }
        return null
    }

    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
    private fun gestureKey(id: Int): String? = when (id) {
        AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_UP -> "swipe_up"
        AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_DOWN -> "swipe_down"
        AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_LEFT -> "swipe_left"
        AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_RIGHT -> "swipe_right"
        AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_LEFT_AND_RIGHT -> "left_right"
        AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_RIGHT_AND_LEFT -> "right_left"
        AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_UP_AND_DOWN -> "up_down"
        AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_DOWN_AND_UP -> "down_up"
        AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_LEFT_AND_UP -> "left_up"
        AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_LEFT_AND_DOWN -> "left_down"
        AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_RIGHT_AND_UP -> "right_up"
        AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_RIGHT_AND_DOWN -> "right_down"
        AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_UP_AND_LEFT -> "up_left"
        AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_UP_AND_RIGHT -> "up_right"
        AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_DOWN_AND_LEFT -> "down_left"
        AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_DOWN_AND_RIGHT -> "down_right"
        AccessibilityService.GESTURE_DOUBLE_TAP_AND_HOLD -> "double_tap_hold"
        else -> GestureConfig.multiFingerIds[id]
    }

    override fun onGesture(gestureId: Int): Boolean {
        if (voiceActive || assistantActive) {
            // A touch normally stops dictation / the assistant, but not while you are still
            // speaking and not in the first moments after it starts listening.
            stopByTouchIfAllowed()
            return true
        }
        if (holdActive) {
            // While a press-and-hold is going on, swipes move the held touch.
            holdSwipe(gestureId)
            return true
        }
        if (gestureId == AccessibilityService.GESTURE_DOUBLE_TAP) {
            if (mode == Mode.NORMAL) activateCurrent() else activateModal()
            return true
        }
        if (mode != Mode.NORMAL) {
            // Inside the menu the gestures are fixed so you can never get stuck.
            when (gestureId) {
                AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_RIGHT -> stepModal(1)
                AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_LEFT -> stepModal(-1)
                AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_DOWN_AND_RIGHT -> closeMenu()
                AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_DOWN_AND_LEFT -> goBack()
            }
            return true
        }
        val key = gestureKey(gestureId) ?: return super.onGesture(gestureId)
        val prefs = getSharedPreferences("screen_reader_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        runFunction(GestureConfig.functionFor(prefs, key))
        return true
    }

    // ---- Double tap and hold: really press and hold the focused item (WhatsApp voice message) ----
    // The finger stays on the glass; we keep a matching held touch on the app until you lift.
    // Swipes while holding move that held touch (slide left to cancel, up to lock, etc.).

    private var holdActive = false
    private var holdStroke: GestureDescription.StrokeDescription? = null
    private var holdX = 0f
    private var holdY = 0f
    private var holdStartedAt = 0L
    private var pendingMove: Pair<Float, Float>? = null

    private fun startHold() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 26) {
            fail("Needs Android 8 or newer")
            return
        }
        val node = focusedNode ?: nodeList.getOrNull(currentIndex)
        if (node == null) {
            fail("Focus an item first")
            return
        }
        val r = boundsOf(node)
        holdX = r.exactCenterX()
        holdY = r.exactCenterY()
        pendingMove = null
        holdStartedAt = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val path = Path()
        path.moveTo(holdX, holdY)
        val stroke = GestureDescription.StrokeDescription(path, 0L, 250L, true)
        holdStroke = stroke
        holdActive = true
        sendHoldStroke(stroke)
        playSound(soundActivate)
    }

    private fun sendHoldStroke(stroke: GestureDescription.StrokeDescription) {
        val g = GestureDescription.Builder().addStroke(stroke).build()
        val ok = dispatchGesture(g, object : AccessibilityService.GestureResultCallback() {
            override fun onCompleted(gestureDescription: GestureDescription?) {
                if (holdActive) handler.post { nextHoldSegment() }
            }

            override fun onCancelled(gestureDescription: GestureDescription?) {
                holdActive = false
                holdStroke = null
            }
        }, null)
        if (!ok) {
            holdActive = false
            holdStroke = null
            fail("Could not hold. Turn the screen reader off and on once in accessibility settings")
        }
    }

    private fun nextHoldSegment() {
        val prev = holdStroke ?: return
        if (!holdActive || Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 26) return
        val mv = pendingMove
        pendingMove = null
        val path = Path()
        path.moveTo(holdX, holdY)
        var duration = 250L
        if (mv != null) {
            path.lineTo(mv.first, mv.second)
            holdX = mv.first
            holdY = mv.second
            duration = 300L
        }
        val timedOut = System.currentTimeMillis() - holdStartedAt > 55000L
        val last = (!touching || timedOut) && mv == null
        val next = prev.continueStroke(path, 0L, duration, !last)
        holdStroke = if (last) null else next
        if (last) holdActive = false
        sendHoldStroke(next)
    }

    private fun holdSwipe(gestureId: Int) {
        val dm = resources.displayMetrics
        val d = 130f * dm.density
        var x = holdX
        var y = holdY
        when (gestureId) {
            AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_UP -> y -= d
            AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_DOWN -> y += d
            AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_LEFT -> x -= d
            AccessibilityService.GESTURE_SWIPE_RIGHT -> x += d
            else -> return
        }
        x = x.coerceIn(1f, (dm.widthPixels - 1).toFloat())
        y = y.coerceIn(1f, (dm.heightPixels - 1).toFloat())
        pendingMove = Pair(x, y)
    }

    private fun globalAction(action: Int) {
        if (performGlobalAction(action)) playSound(soundActivate) else fail("Could not do that")
    }

    // Copy, cut, paste etc. on the focused item, straight from a gesture.
    private fun quickOp(op: (AccessibilityNodeInfo) -> OpResult) {
        val node = focusedNode ?: nodeList.getOrNull(currentIndex)
        if (node == null) {
            fail("Focus an item first")
            return
        }
        val r = op(node)
        if (r.ok) {
            playSound(soundActivate)
            speak(r.msg)
        } else {
            fail(r.msg)
        }
    }

    private fun scrollFromGesture(dir: Int) {
        val from = focusedNode ?: nodeList.getOrNull(currentIndex)
        val scroller = findVerticalScroller(from, dir) ?: findAnyScrollable(rootInActiveWindow)
        if (scroller != null && scrollList(scroller, dir)) {
            playSound(soundFocus)
        } else {
            fail(if (dir > 0) "Cannot scroll forward" else "Cannot scroll back")
        }
    }

    private fun runFunction(id: String) {
        when (id) {
            "none" -> {}
            "next" -> moveNext()
            "previous" -> movePrevious()
            "activate" -> activateCurrent()
            "read_next" -> readStep(1)
            "read_prev" -> readStep(-1)
            "control_next" -> changeReadingControl(1)
            "control_prev" -> changeReadingControl(-1)
            "menu" -> openMenu()
            "back" -> globalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_BACK)
            "home" -> globalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_HOME)
            "recents" -> globalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_RECENTS)
            "notifications" -> globalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_NOTIFICATIONS)
            "quick_settings" -> globalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_QUICK_SETTINGS)
            "power_dialog" -> globalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_POWER_DIALOG)
            "lock_screen" -> if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 28) globalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_LOCK_SCREEN) else fail("Needs Android 9 or newer")
            "screenshot" -> if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 28) globalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_TAKE_SCREENSHOT) else fail("Needs Android 9 or newer")
            "copy" -> quickOp { opCopy(it, false) }
            "append" -> quickOp { opCopy(it, true) }
            "cut" -> quickOp { opCut(it) }
            "paste" -> quickOp { opPaste(it) }
            "clear_edit" -> quickOp { opClear(it) }
            "voice_typing" -> beginVoiceFlow()
            "translate" -> openTranslate()
            "voice_assistant" -> startAssistant()
            "describe_screen" -> runAi(false)
            "scene_camera" -> openCameraAi("scene")
            "object_camera" -> openCameraAi("objects")
            "describe_image" -> {
                menuTarget = focusedNode ?: nodeList.getOrNull(currentIndex)
                runAi(true)
            }
            "hold_drag" -> holdAndDrag()
            "scroll_forward" -> scrollFromGesture(1)
            "scroll_backward" -> scrollFromGesture(-1)
            "repeat" -> if (lastSpoken.isNotEmpty()) speak(lastSpoken) else playSound(soundAlert)
            "stop_speech" -> tts?.stop()
            "volume_up" -> adjustAccessibilityVolume(true)
            "volume_down" -> adjustAccessibilityVolume(false)
            else -> runDynamicFunction(id)
        }
    }

    // Extensions and auto click functions chosen for a gesture.
    private fun runDynamicFunction(id: String) {
        if (id.startsWith("macro:")) {
            val name = id.substring(6)
            val m = AutoClickStore.load(prefsObj()).firstOrNull { it.name == name }
            if (m == null) fail("Function not found") else runMacro(m)
        } else if (id.startsWith("ext:")) {
            val eid = id.substring(4)
            if (eid == "yt_skip") {
                val p = prefsObj()
                p.edit().putBoolean("ext_youtube_skip", !p.getBoolean("ext_youtube_skip", false)).apply()
                playSound(soundActivate)
                speak(extLabel("yt_skip"))
            } else {
                fail("Extension not found")
            }
        }
    }

    // Touch and hold on the focused item, and keep following the finger when it moves
    // (WhatsApp voice message: hold, then swipe up to lock or left to cancel).
    private fun holdAndDrag() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 30) {
            try {
                getTouchInteractionController(Display.DEFAULT_DISPLAY).performLongClickAndStartDrag()
                return
            } catch (t: Throwable) {
                // not available in this state: use the long press below
            }
        }
        val n = focusedNode ?: nodeList.getOrNull(currentIndex)
        if (n == null) {
            fail("Focus an item first")
            return
        }
        // A normal long press on the item (or on the nearest parent that supports it).
        var p: AccessibilityNodeInfo? = n
        var d = 0
        while (p != null && d < 6) {
            if (p.isLongClickable && p.performAction(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_LONG_CLICK)) {
                playSound(soundActivate)
                return
            }
            p = p.parent
            d++
        }
        // Last choice: a held touch on the item. Hold buttons such as the WhatsApp microphone
        // work better when they have focus (the phone's own touch handling is then used).
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26 && touching) {
            startHold()
            return
        }
        fail("This item does not support touch and hold")
    }

    private fun moveNext() = moveBy(1)

    private fun movePrevious() = moveBy(-1)

    // Can this node scroll up/down (vertical lists only) in the given direction?
    private fun canScrollVertically(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo, dir: Int): Boolean {
        val ids = node.actionList.map { it.id }
        val down = ids.contains(AccessibilityNodeInfo.AccessibilityAction.ACTION_SCROLL_DOWN.id)
        val up = ids.contains(AccessibilityNodeInfo.AccessibilityAction.ACTION_SCROLL_UP.id)
        val left = ids.contains(AccessibilityNodeInfo.AccessibilityAction.ACTION_SCROLL_LEFT.id)
        val right = ids.contains(AccessibilityNodeInfo.AccessibilityAction.ACTION_SCROLL_RIGHT.id)
        if (down || up) return if (dir > 0) down else up
        if (left || right) return false // horizontal only (chips, carousels)
        return ids.contains(if (dir > 0) AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_SCROLL_FORWARD else AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_SCROLL_BACKWARD)
    }

    // The outermost vertical list around this item that can still scroll in this direction.
    private fun findVerticalScroller(from: AccessibilityNodeInfo?, dir: Int): AccessibilityNodeInfo? {
        var cur: AccessibilityNodeInfo? = from
        var best: AccessibilityNodeInfo? = null
        var d = 0
        while (cur != null && d < 30) {
            if (cur.isScrollable && canScrollVertically(cur, dir)) best = cur
            cur = cur.parent
            d++
        }
        return best
    }

    private fun scrollList(scroller: AccessibilityNodeInfo, dir: Int): Boolean {
        val ids = scroller.actionList.map { it.id }
        val directional = if (dir > 0) AccessibilityNodeInfo.AccessibilityAction.ACTION_SCROLL_DOWN.id
        else AccessibilityNodeInfo.AccessibilityAction.ACTION_SCROLL_UP.id
        if (ids.contains(directional) && scroller.performAction(directional)) return true
        return scroller.performAction(
            if (dir > 0) AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_SCROLL_FORWARD else AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_SCROLL_BACKWARD
        )
    }

    private fun moveBy(dir: Int) {
        if (busyScrolling) return
        syncList()
        if (nodeList.isEmpty()) {
            playSound(soundAlert)
            speak("No items found on this screen")
            return
        }
        val from = focusedNode ?: nodeList.getOrNull(currentIndex)
        val target = currentIndex + dir
        val nextNode = nodeList.getOrNull(target)
        // If the item after this one is not inside the same vertical list (or there is none)
        // and that list can still scroll, scroll it to bring in more items first.
        val scroller = findVerticalScroller(from, dir)
        val needScroll = scroller != null && (nextNode == null || !isDescendantOf(nextNode, scroller))
        if (!needScroll) {
            if (nextNode != null) {
                currentIndex = target
                announceCurrent()
            } else {
                wrapAround(dir)
            }
            return
        }
        if (scroller != null && scrollList(scroller, dir)) {
            busyScrolling = true
            handler.postDelayed({
                busyScrolling = false
                val found = syncList()
                val next = currentIndex + dir
                if (found && next in nodeList.indices && isDescendantOf(nodeList[next], scroller)) {
                    currentIndex = next
                    announceCurrent()
                } else {
                    // The old item scrolled out of view (or nothing new came): pick the
                    // first (or last) item inside the scrolled list.
                    val inside = nodeList.indices.filter { isDescendantOf(nodeList[it], scroller) }
                    val pick = if (dir > 0) inside.firstOrNull() else inside.lastOrNull()
                    if (pick != null) {
                        currentIndex = pick
                        announceCurrent()
                    } else if (found && next in nodeList.indices) {
                        currentIndex = next
                        announceCurrent()
                    } else {
                        fail(if (dir > 0) "End of list" else "Start of list")
                    }
                }
            }, 700)
            return
        }
        if (nextNode != null) {
            currentIndex = target
            announceCurrent()
        } else {
            wrapAround(dir)
        }
    }

    private fun wrapAround(dir: Int) {
        if (nodeList.isEmpty()) return
        currentIndex = if (dir > 0) 0 else nodeList.size - 1
        announceCurrent()
    }

    // What is spoken for an item: its label, and for sliders also the value and how to change it.
    private fun spokenLabel(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo): String {
        val label = labelOf(node)
        if (isSlider(node)) {
            val value = sliderValueOrNull(node) ?: "value unknown"
            return (label ?: "Slider") + ", slider, " + value + ", swipe up to increase, swipe down to decrease"
        }
        if (label != null) return label
        val deep = mutableListOf<String>()
        deepText(node, deep, 0)
        if (deep.isNotEmpty()) return deep.joinToString(", ")
        return unlabeledName(node)
    }

    // Last resort: every piece of visible text inside the item, whatever it is.
    private fun deepText(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo, out: MutableList<String>, depth: Int) {
        if (depth > 8 || out.size > 10) return
        for (i in 0 until n.childCount) {
            val c = n.getChild(i) ?: continue
            if (!c.isVisibleToUser) continue
            val own = ownLabel(c)
            if (own != null) out.add(own)
            deepText(c, out, depth + 1)
        }
    }

    // For a control that has no text at all, say what kind of control it is.
    private fun unlabeledName(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo): String {
        val cls = node.className?.toString() ?: ""
        return when {
            cls.contains("Button") -> "Unlabeled button"
            cls.contains("ImageView") || cls.contains("Image") -> if (node.isClickable) "Unlabeled button" else "Unlabeled image"
            cls.contains("EditText") -> "Edit box"
            cls.contains("Switch") || cls.contains("Check") -> "Unlabeled switch"
            node.isClickable -> "Unlabeled button"
            else -> "Unlabeled"
        }
    }

    private fun announceCurrent(suffix: String = "") {
        val node = nodeList.getOrNull(currentIndex) ?: return
        focusedNode = node
        resetFocusState()
        node.performAction(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_FOCUS)
        syncNativeTouch(node)
        playSound(if (soundFocusActionable != null && (node.isClickable || node.isLongClickable)) soundFocusActionable else soundFocus)
        speak(spokenLabel(node) + suffix)
    }

    // ======================= Sliders =======================

    private fun isSlider(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        val cls = node.className?.toString() ?: ""
        if (cls.contains("SeekBar") || cls.contains("Slider")) return true
        val ids = node.actionList.map { it.id }
        if (ids.contains(AccessibilityNodeInfo.AccessibilityAction.ACTION_SET_PROGRESS.id)) return true
        val ri = node.rangeInfo
        if (ri != null && (ids.contains(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_SCROLL_FORWARD) ||
                ids.contains(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_SCROLL_BACKWARD))) return true
        return false
    }

    private fun formatMillis(ms: Float): String {
        val total = Math.round(ms / 1000f)
        val h = total / 3600
        val m = (total % 3600) / 60
        val sec = total % 60
        val parts = mutableListOf<String>()
        if (h > 0) parts.add("" + h + (if (h == 1) " hour" else " hours"))
        if (m > 0) parts.add("" + m + (if (m == 1) " minute" else " minutes"))
        if (sec > 0 || parts.isEmpty()) parts.add("" + sec + (if (sec == 1) " second" else " seconds"))
        return parts.joinToString(" ")
    }

    private val timeRegex = Regex("^\\d{1,2}(:\\d{2}){1,2}$")

    private fun collectTimeTexts(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo?, out: MutableList<String>, depth: Int) {
        if (node == null || depth > 4 || out.size >= 2) return
        val t = ownLabel(node)
        if (t != null && timeRegex.matches(t.trim())) out.add(t.trim())
        for (i in 0 until node.childCount) collectTimeTexts(node.getChild(i), out, depth + 1)
    }

    // Players such as YouTube show "0:05" and "10:00" as separate text next to the seek bar.
    private fun nearbyTimeText(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo): String? {
        var cur: AccessibilityNodeInfo? = node.parent
        var level = 0
        while (cur != null && level < 3) {
            val found = mutableListOf<String>()
            collectTimeTexts(cur, found, 0)
            if (found.isNotEmpty()) {
                return if (found.size >= 2) found[0] + " of " + found[1] else found[0]
            }
            cur = cur.parent
            level++
        }
        return null
    }

    // The current value of a slider as text, or null if the app exposes nothing readable.
    private fun sliderValueOrNull(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo): String? {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 30) {
            val sd = node.stateDescription?.toString()
            if (!sd.isNullOrBlank()) return sd
        }
        val ri = node.rangeInfo
        if (ri != null && ri.max > ri.min) {
            val pct = Math.round((ri.current - ri.min) / (ri.max - ri.min) * 100f)
            val range = ri.max - ri.min
            return if (range >= 10000f) {
                "" + pct + " percent, " + formatMillis(ri.current) + " of " + formatMillis(ri.max)
            } else {
                "" + pct + " percent"
            }
        }
        nearbyTimeText(node)?.let { return it }
        return node.text?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
    }

    // increase = true raises the value, false lowers it. Returns false only if nothing worked.
    private fun adjustSlider(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo, increase: Boolean): Boolean {
        node.refresh()
        val ri = node.rangeInfo
        val ids = node.actionList.map { it.id }
        val canSet = ids.contains(AccessibilityNodeInfo.AccessibilityAction.ACTION_SET_PROGRESS.id)
        val canScroll = ids.contains(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_SCROLL_FORWARD) ||
            ids.contains(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_SCROLL_BACKWARD)
        var ok = false
        if (ri != null && ri.max > ri.min) {
            val range = ri.max - ri.min
            if (increase && ri.current >= ri.max) {
                fail("Maximum")
                return true
            }
            if (!increase && ri.current <= ri.min) {
                fail("Minimum")
                return true
            }
            val preferScroll = range > 1000f && canScroll
            if (canSet && !preferScroll) {
                val isInt = ri.type == AccessibilityNodeInfo.RangeInfo.RANGE_TYPE_INT
                val step = if (isInt && range <= 20f) 1f else range / 20f
                var target = ri.current + (if (increase) step else -step)
                target = target.coerceIn(ri.min, ri.max)
                if (isInt) target = Math.round(target).toFloat()
                val args = Bundle()
                args.putFloat(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_ARGUMENT_PROGRESS_VALUE, target)
                ok = node.performAction(AccessibilityNodeInfo.AccessibilityAction.ACTION_SET_PROGRESS.id, args)
            }
        }
        if (!ok) {
            val act = if (increase) AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_SCROLL_FORWARD else AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_SCROLL_BACKWARD
            ok = node.performAction(act)
        }
        if (!ok) return false
        playSound(soundFocus)
        sliderSpoken = false
        sliderWatchUntil = System.currentTimeMillis() + 1500
        // Speak the NEW value. Apps update it a moment after the action, so read it
        // after a short delay, and try once more if nothing readable showed up yet.
        handler.postDelayed({
            if (!sliderSpoken) {
                node.refresh()
                val v = sliderValueOrNull(node)
                if (v != null) {
                    sliderSpoken = true
                    speak(v)
                }
            }
        }, 300)
        handler.postDelayed({
            if (!sliderSpoken) {
                node.refresh()
                sliderSpoken = true
                speak(sliderValueOrNull(node) ?: "Value changed")
            }
        }, 900)
        return true
    }

    // Reading control "Slider": works on the focused slider, else the first slider found
    // on screen, else (if nothing is detected) tries the focused item directly.
    private fun sliderControlStep(increase: Boolean) {
        val cur = focusedNode ?: nodeList.getOrNull(currentIndex)
        var target: AccessibilityNodeInfo? = if (cur != null && isSlider(cur)) cur else null
        if (target == null) {
            rebuildNodeList()
            val idx = nodeList.indexOfFirst { isSlider(it) }
            if (idx >= 0) {
                target = nodeList[idx]
                currentIndex = idx
                focusedNode = target
                target.performAction(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_FOCUS)
            }
        }
        if (target != null) {
            if (!adjustSlider(target, increase)) fail("Could not change the slider")
            return
        }
        if (cur != null && adjustSlider(cur, increase)) return
        fail("No slider found. Focus a slider first")
    }

    private fun activateCurrent() {
        val node = focusedNode ?: nodeList.getOrNull(currentIndex)
        if (node == null) {
            playSound(soundAlert)
            speak("First swipe to or touch an item")
            return
        }
        if (readingIndex == actionsControlIndex && actionIndex >= 0) {
            val chosen = actionsFor(node).getOrNull(actionIndex)
            if (chosen != null) {
                val r = runEditAction(node, chosen)
                if (r.ok) {
                    playSound(soundActivate)
                    speak(r.msg)
                } else {
                    fail(r.msg)
                }
                return
            }
        }
        val didClick = clickNodeOrAncestor(node)
        if (didClick) {
            playSound(soundActivate)
        } else {
            playSound(soundAlert)
        }
    }

    // ======================= Reading controls =======================

    private fun resetReading() {
        readUnits = emptyList()
        readIndex = -1
        actionIndex = -1
    }

    // Called whenever focus moves to a different item: also ends selection mode.
    private fun resetFocusState() {
        resetReading()
        selecting = false
    }

    // ---- Reading controls Heading / Link / Edit box: swipe down = next one, swipe up = previous one ----

    private fun roleExtra(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): String {
        val ex = n.extras ?: return ""
        val a = ex.getCharSequence("AccessibilityNodeInfo.roleDescription")?.toString() ?: ""
        val b = ex.getCharSequence("AccessibilityNodeInfo.chromeRole")?.toString() ?: ""
        return a + " " + b
    }

    private fun isHeadingNode(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 28 && n.isHeading) return true
        val ci = n.collectionItemInfo
        if (ci != null && ci.isHeading) return true
        return roleExtra(n).contains("heading", true)
    }

    private fun isLinkNode(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        if (roleExtra(n).contains("link", true)) return true
        val cls = n.className?.toString() ?: ""
        if (cls.contains("Link", true) && !cls.contains("Linear", true)) return true
        val t = n.text
        if (t is Spanned && t.length > 0 && t.getSpans(0, t.length, URLSpan::class.java).isNotEmpty()) return true
        return false
    }

    private fun isEditBoxNode(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        return n.isEditable || (n.className?.toString() ?: "").contains("EditText")
    }

    private fun matchesType(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo, kind: Int): Boolean = when (kind) {
        headingControlIndex -> isHeadingNode(n)
        linkControlIndex -> isLinkNode(n)
        else -> isEditBoxNode(n)
    }

    private fun typeWord(kind: Int): String = when (kind) {
        headingControlIndex -> "heading"
        linkControlIndex -> "link"
        else -> "edit box"
    }

    // Jumps to the next / previous heading, link or edit box. If there is none on the screen,
    // the list is scrolled a few times to look further.
    private fun jumpToType(kind: Int, dir: Int, attempt: Int) {
        if (attempt == 0 && busyScrolling) return
        syncList()
        if (nodeList.isEmpty()) {
            busyScrolling = false
            playSound(soundAlert)
            speak("No items found on this screen")
            return
        }
        val cur = focusedNode
        val fi = if (cur != null) nodeList.indexOfFirst { it == cur } else -1
        var i = when {
            attempt == 0 -> currentIndex + dir
            fi >= 0 -> fi + dir
            dir > 0 -> 0
            else -> nodeList.size - 1
        }
        while (i in nodeList.indices) {
            if (matchesType(nodeList[i], kind)) {
                busyScrolling = false
                currentIndex = i
                announceCurrent(", " + typeWord(kind))
                return
            }
            i += dir
        }
        val from = cur ?: nodeList.getOrNull(currentIndex)
        val scroller = findVerticalScroller(from, dir)
        if (attempt < 8 && scroller != null && scrollList(scroller, dir)) {
            busyScrolling = true
            handler.postDelayed({ jumpToType(kind, dir, attempt + 1) }, 700)
            return
        }
        busyScrolling = false
        fail("No " + (if (dir > 0) "next " else "previous ") + typeWord(kind))
    }

    private fun changeReadingControl(dir: Int) {
        // Only the reading controls switched on in "Customize reading controls" are used.
        val off = MenuConfig.disabled(getSharedPreferences("screen_reader_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE), "reading")
        val ids = listOf("normal", "character", "word", "line", "paragraph", "actions", "slider", "heading", "link", "editbox")
        val enabled = ids.indices.filter { it == 0 || !off.contains(ids[it]) }
        val pos = enabled.indexOf(readingIndex).let { if (it < 0) 0 else it }
        readingIndex = enabled[wrap(pos + dir, enabled.size)]
        resetReading()
        playSound(soundFocus)
        speak(readingLabels[readingIndex])
    }

    private fun nodeText(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo): String? {
        node.refresh()
        val t = if (node.isShowingHintText) null else node.text?.toString()
        return t?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
            ?: node.contentDescription?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
            ?: mergedText(node)
    }

    private fun readStep(dir: Int) {
        // Swipe up = increase, swipe down = decrease (dir is +1 for down).
        if (readingIndex >= headingControlIndex) {
            jumpToType(readingIndex, dir, 0)
            return
        }
        if (readingIndex == sliderControlIndex) {
            sliderControlStep(dir < 0)
            return
        }
        if (readingIndex == 0) {
            val focused = focusedNode ?: nodeList.getOrNull(currentIndex)
            if (focused != null && isSlider(focused)) {
                if (!adjustSlider(focused, dir < 0)) fail("Could not change the slider")
                return
            }
            fail("Normal item. Swipe down then up to choose character, word, line, paragraph, actions or slider")
            return
        }
        val node = focusedNode ?: nodeList.getOrNull(currentIndex)
        if (node == null) {
            fail("Focus an item first")
            return
        }
        if (readingIndex == actionsControlIndex) {
            stepAction(node, dir)
            return
        }
        val kind = SplitKind.values()[readingIndex - 1]
        // Edit boxes: swipes move the real cursor (and extend the selection in selection mode).
        if (node.isEditable) {
            cursorStep(node, kind, dir)
            return
        }
        if (readUnits.isEmpty()) {
            val text = nodeText(node)
            if (text == null) {
                fail("This item has no readable text")
                return
            }
            readUnits = splitText(text, kind, true)
            if (readUnits.isEmpty()) {
                fail("This item has no readable text")
                return
            }
            readIndex = if (dir > 0) -1 else readUnits.size
        }
        val next = readIndex + dir
        if (next < 0) {
            fail("Start")
            return
        }
        if (next >= readUnits.size) {
            fail("End")
            return
        }
        readIndex = next
        playSound(soundFocus)
        speak(speakable(readUnits[next]))
    }

    private fun clickNodeOrAncestor(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        node.refresh()
        var cur: AccessibilityNodeInfo? = node
        var depth = 0
        while (cur != null && depth < 6) {
            if (cur.isClickable && cur.performAction(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_CLICK)) return true
            cur = cur.parent
            depth++
        }
        return node.performAction(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_CLICK)
    }

    private fun hintModalEntry() {
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        if (now - lastModalHoverHintAt < 2500) return
        lastModalHoverHintAt = now
        val current = when (mode) {
            Mode.MENU -> menuLabels[menuIndex]
            Mode.UNIT_CHOOSER -> unitLabels[unitIndex]
            Mode.ITEM_SELECT -> describeSelectEntry(selectIndex)
            Mode.AI_MENU -> aiLabels[aiIndex]
            Mode.EXT_MENU -> extLabel(extIds.getOrNull(extIndex) ?: "back")
            Mode.VOICE_MENU -> voiceLabels.getOrElse(voiceIndex) { "" }
            Mode.NORMAL -> ""
        }
        speak("Menu is open. Swipe left or right to change, double tap to choose. Now: " + current)
    }

    // ======================= Screen Reader Menu =======================

    private fun wrap(i: Int, n: Int): Int = ((i % n) + n) % n

    private fun openMenu() {
        menuTarget = focusedNode ?: nodeList.getOrNull(currentIndex)
        refreshMenuIds()
        mode = Mode.MENU
        menuIndex = 0
        playSound(soundScreenChange)
        speak("Screen reader menu. Swipe left or right to change options, double tap to choose. " + menuLabels[0])
    }

    private fun closeMenu() {
        mode = Mode.NORMAL
        menuTarget = null
        playSound(soundScreenChange)
        speak("Menu closed")
    }

    private fun goBack() {
        when (mode) {
            Mode.ITEM_SELECT -> {
                mode = Mode.UNIT_CHOOSER
                playSound(soundFocus)
                speak("Choose unit. " + unitLabels[unitIndex])
            }
            Mode.UNIT_CHOOSER -> {
                mode = Mode.MENU
                playSound(soundFocus)
                speak("Menu. " + menuLabels[menuIndex])
            }
            Mode.VOICE_MENU -> {
                mode = Mode.AI_MENU
                playSound(soundFocus)
                speak("AI menu. " + aiLabels[aiIndex])
            }
            Mode.AI_MENU, Mode.EXT_MENU -> {
                mode = Mode.MENU
                playSound(soundFocus)
                speak("Menu. " + menuLabels[menuIndex])
            }
            Mode.MENU -> closeMenu()
            Mode.NORMAL -> {}
        }
    }

    private fun stepModal(dir: Int) {
        playSound(soundFocus)
        when (mode) {
            Mode.MENU -> {
                menuIndex = wrap(menuIndex + dir, menuLabels.size)
                speak(menuLabels[menuIndex])
            }
            Mode.UNIT_CHOOSER -> {
                unitIndex = wrap(unitIndex + dir, unitLabels.size)
                speak(unitLabels[unitIndex])
            }
            Mode.ITEM_SELECT -> {
                selectIndex = wrap(selectIndex + dir, selectUnits.size + 2)
                speak(describeSelectEntry(selectIndex))
            }
            Mode.AI_MENU -> {
                aiIndex = wrap(aiIndex + dir, aiLabels.size)
                speak(aiLabels[aiIndex])
            }
            Mode.EXT_MENU -> {
                extIndex = wrap(extIndex + dir, extIds.size)
                speak(extLabel(extIds[extIndex]))
            }
            Mode.VOICE_MENU -> {
                voiceIndex = wrap(voiceIndex + dir, voiceLabels.size)
                speak(voiceLabels[voiceIndex])
            }
            Mode.NORMAL -> {}
        }
    }

    private fun activateModal() {
        when (mode) {
            Mode.MENU -> when (menuIds.getOrNull(menuIndex)) {
                "copy" -> doCopy()
                "append" -> doAppend()
                "cut" -> doCut()
                "paste" -> doPaste()
                "select" -> startSelect()
                "clear" -> doClear()
                "ai" -> openAiMenu()
                "extensions" -> openExtMenu()
                "translate" -> openTranslate()
                "assistant" -> startAssistant()
                else -> closeMenu()
            }
            Mode.EXT_MENU -> activateExt()
            Mode.VOICE_MENU -> activateVoiceMenu()
            Mode.AI_MENU -> when (aiIndex) {
                0 -> runAi(false)
                1 -> runAi(true)
                2 -> beginVoiceFlow()
                3 -> openCameraAi("scene")
                4 -> openCameraAi("objects")
                else -> goBack()
            }
            Mode.UNIT_CHOOSER -> beginItemSelect(SplitKind.values()[unitIndex])
            Mode.ITEM_SELECT -> {
                val n = selectUnits.size
                when {
                    selectIndex < n -> toggleSelected(selectIndex)
                    selectIndex == n -> copySelection(false)
                    else -> copySelection(true)
                }
            }
            Mode.NORMAL -> {}
        }
    }

    private fun fail(message: String) {
        playSound(soundAlert)
        speak(message)
    }

    private fun finish(message: String) {
        mode = Mode.NORMAL
        menuTarget = null
        playSound(soundActivate)
        speak(message)
    }

    private fun targetText(): String? {
        val node = menuTarget
        if (node == null) {
            fail("Focus an item first, then open the menu")
            return null
        }
        node.refresh()
        val t = if (node.isShowingHintText) null else node.text?.toString()
        val s = t?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
            ?: node.contentDescription?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
            ?: mergedText(node)
        if (s == null) {
            fail("This item has no text that can be copied")
            return null
        }
        return s
    }

    private fun writeClipboard(): Boolean {
        return try {
            val cm = getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE) as ClipboardManager
            cm.setPrimaryClip(ClipData.newPlainText("screen_reader", clipList.joinToString("\n")))
            suppressWindowEventsUntil = System.currentTimeMillis() + 2000
            true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    // ======================= Auto focus on a new screen =======================

    private fun scheduleAutoFocus(title: String) {
        autoFocusPending?.let { handler.removeCallbacks(it) }
        val r = Runnable { autoFocus(title) }
        autoFocusPending = r
        handler.postDelayed(r, 350)
    }

    // Instead of announcing "new screen", put focus somewhere sensible and read it.
    private fun autoFocus(title: String) {
        if (mode != Mode.NORMAL || gamingActive) return
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        if (now - lastAutoFocusAt < 1200) return
        lastAutoFocusAt = now
        rebuildNodeList()
        currentIndex = -1
        focusedNode = null
        resetFocusState()
        playSound(soundScreenChange)
        if (nodeList.isEmpty()) {
            if (title.isNotEmpty()) speak(title)
            return
        }
        var idx = 0
        try {
            val input = rootInActiveWindow?.findFocus(AccessibilityNodeInfo.FOCUS_INPUT)
            if (input != null) {
                val i = nodeList.indexOfFirst { it == input }
                if (i >= 0) idx = i
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // keep first item
        }
        currentIndex = idx
        val node = nodeList[idx]
        focusedNode = node
        node.performAction(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_FOCUS)
        val label = spokenLabel(node)
        speak(if (title.isNotEmpty() && title != label) "$title. $label" else label)
    }

    // ======================= Typing feedback =======================

    private fun typedCharText(c: Char): String = when {
        c.isUpperCase() -> "capital $c"
        c == ' ' -> "space"
        c == '\n' -> "new line"
        else -> c.toString()
    }

    private fun echoTyping(event: AccessibilityEvent) {
        if (voiceActive) return
        if (event.isPassword) return
        val now = event.text.firstOrNull()?.toString() ?: return
        val from = event.fromIndex
        val added = event.addedCount
        val removed = event.removedCount
        if (added > 0 && from >= 0 && from + added <= now.length) {
            val ins = now.substring(from, from + added)
            if (ins.length == 1) {
                val c = ins[0]
                if (c == ' ' || c == '\n') {
                    // Space or Enter: say the word that was just finished.
                    val before = now.substring(0, from).trimEnd()
                    val word = before.split(' ', '\n').lastOrNull() ?: ""
                    speak(if (word.isNotBlank()) word else typedCharText(c))
                } else {
                    speak(typedCharText(c))
                }
            } else if (ins.isNotBlank()) {
                speak(if (ins.length > 300) ins.substring(0, 300) else ins.trim())
            }
        } else if (removed > 0 && added == 0) {
            val before = event.beforeText?.toString()
            if (before != null && from >= 0 && from < before.length) {
                val gone = before.substring(from, minOf(before.length, from + removed))
                speak(if (gone.length == 1) "deleted " + typedCharText(gone[0]) else "deleted")
            } else {
                speak("deleted")
            }
        }
    }

    // ======================= Gaming mode =======================

    private fun isGamePackage(pkg: String): Boolean {
        if (pkg == packageName) return false
        val prefs = getSharedPreferences("screen_reader_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val on = prefs.getStringSet("gaming_on", emptySet()) ?: emptySet()
        val off = prefs.getStringSet("gaming_off", emptySet()) ?: emptySet()
        if (off.contains(pkg)) return false
        if (on.contains(pkg)) return true
        return try {
            packageManager.getApplicationInfo(pkg, 0).category == ApplicationInfo.CATEGORY_GAME
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    private fun updateGamingMode(pkg: String?) {
        if (pkg == null || pkg == packageName || pkg == "com.android.systemui") return
        if (pkg == lastForegroundPackage) return
        lastForegroundPackage = pkg
        if (gamingSuppressedPackage != null && gamingSuppressedPackage != pkg) gamingSuppressedPackage = null
        val want = gamingSuppressedPackage != pkg && isGamePackage(pkg)
        if (want && !gamingActive) enterGaming() else if (!want && gamingActive) exitGaming()
    }

    // Press-and-hold buttons (WhatsApp voice message and similar): while one of them has focus the
    // phone's own double tap handling is used, exactly like TalkBack does. Double tap clicks it and
    // double tap and hold passes your finger straight to the app, so slide up to lock works.
    private var nativeTouchOn = false

    private fun wantsNativeTouch(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Boolean {
        val id = node.viewIdResourceName?.lowercase() ?: ""
        if (id.contains("voice_note") || id.contains("record_btn") || id.contains("record_button") ||
            id.contains("mic_btn") || id.contains("mic_button") || id.contains("voice_btn") ||
            id.contains("voice_message") || id.contains("audio_record")) return true
        val t = ((node.contentDescription?.toString() ?: "") + " " + (node.text?.toString() ?: "")).lowercase()
        return t.contains("voice message") || t.contains("voice note") || t.contains("record voice") ||
            t.contains("hold to record") || t.contains("hold to talk") || t.contains("microphone") ||
            t.contains("audio message") || t.contains("record audio") || t.contains("push to talk")
    }

    private fun syncNativeTouch(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo?) {
        val want = !gamingActive && node != null && wantsNativeTouch(node)
        if (want == nativeTouchOn) return
        nativeTouchOn = want
        try {
            val info = serviceInfo ?: return
            val f = AccessibilityServiceInfo.FLAG_SERVICE_HANDLES_DOUBLE_TAP
            info.flags = if (want) info.flags and f.inv() else info.flags or f
            serviceInfo = info
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // ignore
        }
    }

    private fun setTouchExploration(on: Boolean) {
        nativeTouchOn = false
        try {
            val info = serviceInfo ?: return
            val f = AccessibilityServiceInfo.FLAG_REQUEST_TOUCH_EXPLORATION_MODE or
                AccessibilityServiceInfo.FLAG_SERVICE_HANDLES_DOUBLE_TAP or
                AccessibilityServiceInfo.FLAG_REQUEST_MULTI_FINGER_GESTURES
            info.flags = if (on) info.flags or f else info.flags and f.inv()
            serviceInfo = info
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // ignore
        }
    }

    private fun enterGaming() {
        gamingActive = true
        mode = Mode.NORMAL
        menuTarget = null
        autoFocusPending?.let { handler.removeCallbacks(it) }
        setTouchExploration(false)
        playSound(soundScreenChange)
        speak("Gaming mode on. Screen reader touch gestures are off. Press both volume keys together to turn it off.")
    }

    private fun exitGaming() {
        gamingActive = false
        setTouchExploration(true)
        playSound(soundScreenChange)
        speak("Gaming mode off")
    }

    // In gaming mode there is no touch exploration, so read text the game itself puts on screen.
    private fun speakGameText(event: AccessibilityEvent) {
        val types = event.contentChangeTypes
        val textual = (types and AccessibilityEvent.CONTENT_CHANGE_TYPE_TEXT) != 0 ||
            (types and AccessibilityEvent.CONTENT_CHANGE_TYPE_CONTENT_DESCRIPTION) != 0
        if (!textual) return
        var t = event.text.joinToString(" ").trim()
        if (t.isEmpty()) t = event.contentDescription?.toString()?.trim() ?: ""
        if (t.isEmpty()) return
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        if (t == lastGameText && now - lastGameTextAt < 1000) return
        lastGameText = t
        lastGameTextAt = now
        speak(t)
    }

    // ======================= Extensions =======================

    private fun prefsObj() = getSharedPreferences("screen_reader_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    private fun extLabel(id: String): String {
        if (id == "yt_skip") {
            return "Skip ads on YouTube: " + (if (prefsObj().getBoolean("ext_youtube_skip", false)) "On" else "Off")
        }
        if (id.startsWith("macro:")) {
            val idx = id.substring(6).toIntOrNull() ?: -1
            val list = AutoClickStore.load(prefsObj())
            return "Run function: " + (list.getOrNull(idx)?.name ?: "unknown")
        }
        return "Back"
    }

    private fun openExtMenu() {
        val macros = AutoClickStore.load(prefsObj())
        extIds = listOf("yt_skip") + macros.indices.map { "macro:" + it } + "back"
        extIndex = 0
        mode = Mode.EXT_MENU
        playSound(soundScreenChange)
        speak("Extensions. Swipe left or right, double tap to choose. " + extLabel(extIds[0]))
    }

    private fun activateExt() {
        val id = extIds.getOrNull(extIndex) ?: return
        when {
            id == "yt_skip" -> {
                val p = prefsObj()
                p.edit().putBoolean("ext_youtube_skip", !p.getBoolean("ext_youtube_skip", false)).apply()
                playSound(soundActivate)
                speak(extLabel(id))
            }
            id.startsWith("macro:") -> {
                val idx = id.substring(6).toIntOrNull() ?: -1
                val m = AutoClickStore.load(prefsObj()).getOrNull(idx)
                if (m == null) fail("Function not found") else runMacro(m)
            }
            else -> goBack()
        }
    }

    // ---- YouTube: press "Skip ad" automatically ----

    private val skipLoop = object : Runnable {
        override fun run() {
            try {
                if (!gamingActive && lastForegroundPackage == youtubePackage &&
                    prefsObj().getBoolean("ext_youtube_skip", false)
                ) {
                    trySkipAd()
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                // never let the loop die
            }
            handler.postDelayed(this, 1000)
        }
    }

    private fun nfc(s: String): String = Normalizer.normalize(s, Normalizer.Form.NFC).lowercase()

    private fun isSkipAdLabel(raw: String): Boolean {
        val l = nfc(raw).trim()
        if (l == "skip" || l == "skip ad" || l == "skip ads" || l.startsWith("skip ad")) return true
        return l.contains(nfc("\u0935\u093f\u091c\u094d\u091e\u093e\u092a\u0928")) && l.contains(nfc("\u091b\u094b\u0921\u093c"))
    }

    private fun trySkipAd() {
        val root = rootInActiveWindow ?: return
        if (root.packageName?.toString() != youtubePackage) return
        val candidates = mutableListOf<AccessibilityNodeInfo>()
        candidates.addAll(root.findAccessibilityNodeInfosByText("skip"))
        candidates.addAll(root.findAccessibilityNodeInfosByText("\u0935\u093f\u091c\u094d\u091e\u093e\u092a\u0928"))
        for (c in candidates) {
            if (!c.isVisibleToUser) continue
            val labels = listOfNotNull(ownLabel(c), c.contentDescription?.toString())
            if (labels.any { isSkipAdLabel(it) }) {
                if (clickNodeOrAncestor(c)) {
                    playSound(soundActivate)
                    speak("Ad skipped")
                    return
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // ---- Auto click functions ----

    private fun normLabel(s: String): String = s.trim().replace(Regex("\\s+"), " ").lowercase()

    private fun labelsOfNode(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): List<String> {
        val l = mutableListOf<String>()
        ownLabel(n)?.let { l.add(it) }
        n.contentDescription?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }?.let { l.add(it) }
        labelOf(n)?.let { l.add(it) }
        mergedText(n)?.let { l.add(it) }
        return l.map { normLabel(it) }
    }

    // Finds the item copied from the screen: an exact match first, then the shortest label containing it.
    private fun findStepNode(step: String, avoid: AccessibilityNodeInfo?, allowAvoid: Boolean): AccessibilityNodeInfo? {
        rebuildNodeList()
        val want = normLabel(step)
        var partial: AccessibilityNodeInfo? = null
        var partialLen = Int.MAX_VALUE
        for (n in nodeList) {
            if (avoid != null && n == avoid && !allowAvoid) continue
            val labels = labelsOfNode(n)
            if (labels.any { it == want }) return n
            val shortest = labels.filter { it.contains(want) }.minOfOrNull { it.length }
            if (shortest != null && shortest < partialLen) {
                partial = n
                partialLen = shortest
            }
        }
        return partial
    }

    private fun runMacro(m: Macro) {
        if (m.steps.isEmpty()) {
            fail("This function has no steps")
            return
        }
        if (macroRunning) {
            fail("A function is already running")
            return
        }
        macroRunning = true
        mode = Mode.NORMAL
        menuTarget = null
        playSound(soundActivate)
        speak("Running " + m.name)
        handler.postDelayed({ macroStep(m.steps, 0, null, System.currentTimeMillis()) }, 1200)
    }

    private fun macroStep(steps: List<String>, idx: Int, last: AccessibilityNodeInfo?, since: Long) {
        if (idx >= steps.size) {
            macroRunning = false
            playSound(soundActivate)
            speak("Function finished")
            return
        }
        val elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - since
        // If only the item we just clicked matches, wait a little in case the screen is still changing.
        val node = findStepNode(steps[idx], last, elapsed > 1500)
        if (node != null) {
            clickNodeOrAncestor(node)
            playSound(soundFocus)
            handler.postDelayed({ macroStep(steps, idx + 1, node, System.currentTimeMillis()) }, 900)
            return
        }
        if (elapsed > 5000) {
            macroRunning = false
            fail("Could not find " + steps[idx] + " on the screen. Stopped at step " + (idx + 1))
            return
        }
        handler.postDelayed({ macroStep(steps, idx, last, since) }, 300)
    }

    // ======================= Proximity sensor =======================

    // Covering the proximity sensor silences speech (can be switched off on the app's home screen).
    private fun startProximity() {
        try {
            if (proximityListener != null) return
            val sm = getSystemService(Context.SENSOR_SERVICE) as SensorManager
            val sensor = sm.getDefaultSensor(Sensor.TYPE_PROXIMITY) ?: return
            val listener = object : SensorEventListener {
                override fun onSensorChanged(event: SensorEvent) {
                    if (!prefsObj().getBoolean("proximity_silence", true)) return
                    val near = event.values.isNotEmpty() && event.values[0] < minOf(sensor.maximumRange, 5f)
                    if (near) {
                        try {
                            tts?.stop()
                        } catch (e: Exception) {
                            // ignore
                        }
                    }
                }

                override fun onAccuracyChanged(sensor: Sensor?, accuracy: Int) {}
            }
            proximityListener = listener
            sm.registerListener(listener, sensor, SensorManager.SENSOR_DELAY_NORMAL)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // no sensor
        }
    }

    // ======================= Translate popup =======================

    private fun openTranslate() {
        mode = Mode.NORMAL
        menuTarget = null
        playSound(soundScreenChange)
        try {
            startActivity(Intent(this, TranslateActivity::class.java).addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK))
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            fail("Could not open the translate window.")
        }
    }

    // ======================= Voice assistant (on-device speech recognition) =======================

    // What you can say, mapped to the same functions a gesture can run.
    private val voiceCommands: Map<String, String> = mapOf(
        "next" to "next", "next item" to "next",
        "previous" to "previous", "previous item" to "previous",
        "activate" to "activate", "click" to "activate", "double tap" to "activate",
        "read next" to "read_next", "next part" to "read_next",
        "read previous" to "read_prev", "previous part" to "read_prev",
        "next reading control" to "control_next", "next control" to "control_next",
        "previous reading control" to "control_prev", "previous control" to "control_prev",
        "menu" to "menu", "open menu" to "menu", "screen reader menu" to "menu",
        "back" to "back", "go back" to "back", "press back" to "back",
        "home" to "home", "go home" to "home", "home screen" to "home",
        "recents" to "recents", "recent apps" to "recents", "overview" to "recents", "show recent apps" to "recents",
        "notifications" to "notifications", "open notifications" to "notifications", "show notifications" to "notifications",
        "quick settings" to "quick_settings", "open quick settings" to "quick_settings",
        "power menu" to "power_dialog", "power dialog" to "power_dialog",
        "lock screen" to "lock_screen", "lock phone" to "lock_screen",
        "screenshot" to "screenshot", "take screenshot" to "screenshot", "take a screenshot" to "screenshot",
        "copy" to "copy", "copy this" to "copy",
        "append" to "append",
        "cut" to "cut",
        "paste" to "paste",
        "clear text" to "clear_edit", "clear edit box" to "clear_edit", "clear field" to "clear_edit",
        "describe screen" to "describe_screen", "describe the screen" to "describe_screen",
        "describe image" to "describe_image", "describe this image" to "describe_image",
        "voice typing" to "voice_typing", "start typing" to "voice_typing", "dictate" to "voice_typing",
        "translate" to "translate", "open translate" to "translate",
        "scroll down" to "scroll_forward", "scroll forward" to "scroll_forward", "scroll" to "scroll_forward",
        "scroll up" to "scroll_backward", "scroll back" to "scroll_backward",
        "repeat" to "repeat", "say again" to "repeat", "repeat that" to "repeat",
        "stop" to "stop_speech", "stop speaking" to "stop_speech", "be quiet" to "stop_speech", "quiet" to "stop_speech",
        "volume up" to "volume_up", "speech volume up" to "volume_up",
        "volume down" to "volume_down", "speech volume down" to "volume_down"
    )

    private fun cleanPhrase(raw: String): String {
        var t = raw.lowercase().replace(Regex("[^\\p{L}\\p{N}]+"), " ").trim()
        for (pre in listOf("please ", "ok ", "okay ", "hey ")) {
            if (t.startsWith(pre)) t = t.substring(pre.length)
        }
        if (t.endsWith(" please")) t = t.substring(0, t.length - 7)
        return t.trim()
    }

    private fun squash(s: String): String = s.lowercase().filter { it.isLetterOrDigit() }

    private fun startAssistant() {
        mode = Mode.NORMAL
        menuTarget = null
        if (assistantActive) {
            stopAssistant()
            return
        }
        if (voiceActive) {
            fail("Voice typing is running. Touch the screen to stop it first.")
            return
        }
        if (checkSelfPermission(Manifest.permission.RECORD_AUDIO) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
            speak("Microphone permission is needed. A permission screen will open. Allow it, then start the voice assistant again.")
            try {
                startActivity(Intent(this, MicPermissionActivity::class.java).addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK))
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                fail("Could not open the permission screen.")
            }
            return
        }
        if (!SpeechRecognizer.isRecognitionAvailable(this)) {
            fail("Speech recognition is not available on this phone.")
            return
        }
        assistantActive = true
        assistantHearing = false
        assistantTouchOkAfter = Long.MAX_VALUE
        speak("Listening. Say a command.")
        afterSpeech {
            if (assistantActive) beginRecognition()
        }
    }

    private fun stopByTouchIfAllowed() {
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        if (voiceActive) {
            val pausedLongEnough = now - voiceLastSpeechAt > 1500
            if (now >= voiceTouchOkAfter && pausedLongEnough) stopVoiceTyping()
        }
        if (assistantActive) {
            if (now >= assistantTouchOkAfter && !assistantHearing) stopAssistant()
        }
    }

    private fun stopAssistant() {
        if (!assistantActive) return
        playSound(soundActivate)
        finishAssistant()
    }

    private fun finishAssistant() {
        assistantActive = false
        val r = recognizer
        recognizer = null
        if (r != null) {
            handler.post {
                try {
                    r.cancel()
                    r.destroy()
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    // ignore
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun beginRecognition() {
        try {
            val onDevice = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33 && SpeechRecognizer.isOnDeviceRecognitionAvailable(this)
            val r = if (onDevice) SpeechRecognizer.createOnDeviceSpeechRecognizer(this) else SpeechRecognizer.createSpeechRecognizer(this)
            recognizer = r
            r.setRecognitionListener(object : RecognitionListener {
                override fun onReadyForSpeech(params: Bundle?) {}
                override fun onBeginningOfSpeech() {
                    assistantHearing = true
                }
                override fun onRmsChanged(rmsdB: Float) {}
                override fun onBufferReceived(buffer: ByteArray?) {}
                override fun onEndOfSpeech() {
                    assistantHearing = false
                    assistantTouchOkAfter = System.currentTimeMillis() + 1500
                }
                override fun onPartialResults(partialResults: Bundle?) {}
                override fun onEvent(eventType: Int, params: Bundle?) {}

                override fun onError(error: Int) {
                    if (!assistantActive) return
                    finishAssistant()
                    playSound(soundAlert)
                    speak(
                        when (error) {
                            SpeechRecognizer.ERROR_NO_MATCH, SpeechRecognizer.ERROR_SPEECH_TIMEOUT -> "I did not hear a command."
                            SpeechRecognizer.ERROR_LANGUAGE_NOT_SUPPORTED, SpeechRecognizer.ERROR_LANGUAGE_UNAVAILABLE,
                            SpeechRecognizer.ERROR_NETWORK, SpeechRecognizer.ERROR_NETWORK_TIMEOUT, SpeechRecognizer.ERROR_SERVER ->
                                "Offline English speech recognition is not ready. Download the English offline speech pack in Google speech settings, then try again."
                            SpeechRecognizer.ERROR_INSUFFICIENT_PERMISSIONS -> "Microphone permission is missing."
                            SpeechRecognizer.ERROR_RECOGNIZER_BUSY -> "The speech recognizer is busy. Try again."
                            else -> "Speech recognition error " + error
                        }
                    )
                }

                override fun onResults(results: Bundle?) {
                    if (!assistantActive) return
                    val list = results?.getStringArrayList(SpeechRecognizer.RESULTS_RECOGNITION) ?: arrayListOf()
                    finishAssistant()
                    handleCommand(list)
                }
            })
            val intent = Intent(RecognizerIntent.ACTION_RECOGNIZE_SPEECH)
            intent.putExtra(RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_LANGUAGE_MODEL, RecognizerIntent.LANGUAGE_MODEL_FREE_FORM)
            intent.putExtra(RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_LANGUAGE, "en-IN")
            intent.putExtra(RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_PREFER_OFFLINE, true)
            intent.putExtra(RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_MAX_RESULTS, 5)
            playSound(soundFocus)
            assistantTouchOkAfter = System.currentTimeMillis() + 4000
            r.startListening(intent)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            finishAssistant()
            fail("Could not start speech recognition.")
        }
    }

    // The item on screen whose name matches exactly (ignoring case and punctuation).
    private fun findExactNode(name: String): AccessibilityNodeInfo? {
        rebuildNodeList()
        val want = squash(name)
        if (want.isEmpty()) return null
        for (n in nodeList) {
            if (labelsOfNode(n).any { squash(it) == want }) return n
        }
        return null
    }

    private fun clickTarget(p: String): String? {
        for (pre in listOf("click on ", "click ", "tap on ", "tap ", "press ", "select ")) {
            if (p.startsWith(pre) && p.length > pre.length) return p.substring(pre.length).trim()
        }
        return null
    }

    private fun handleCommand(alternatives: List<String>) {
        val phrases = alternatives.map { cleanPhrase(it) }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
        if (phrases.isEmpty()) {
            playSound(soundAlert)
            speak("I did not catch that.")
            return
        }
        // 1. The same functions a gesture can run.
        for (p in phrases) {
            val id = voiceCommands[p]
            if (id != null) {
                playSound(soundActivate)
                runFunction(id)
                return
            }
        }
        // 2. The Skip ads extension: "skip ad", optionally with "on" or "off".
        for (p0 in phrases) {
            val p = p0.replace("skip adds", "skip ad").replace("skip ads", "skip ad").replace("skip add", "skip ad")
            if (p.contains("skip ad")) {
                val words = p.split(" ")
                val prefs = prefsObj()
                val now = prefs.getBoolean("ext_youtube_skip", false)
                val want = if (words.contains("off")) false else if (words.contains("on")) true else !now
                prefs.edit().putBoolean("ext_youtube_skip", want).apply()
                playSound(soundActivate)
                speak(extLabel("yt_skip"))
                return
            }
        }
        // 3. An auto click function, by the name it was saved with.
        val macros = AutoClickStore.load(prefsObj())
        for (p in phrases) {
            for (m in macros) {
                val n = cleanPhrase(m.name)
                if (n.isEmpty()) continue
                if (p == n || p == "run " + n || p == "start " + n || p == "execute " + n || p == "activate " + n || p == "do " + n) {
                    runMacro(m)
                    return
                }
            }
        }
        // 4. "click <exact name of an item on the screen>".
        var firstTarget: String? = null
        for (p in phrases) {
            val t = clickTarget(p) ?: continue
            if (firstTarget == null) firstTarget = t
            val node = findExactNode(t)
            if (node != null) {
                if (clickNodeOrAncestor(node)) {
                    playSound(soundActivate)
                    speak("Clicked " + t)
                } else {
                    fail("Found " + t + " but it could not be clicked")
                }
                return
            }
        }
        if (firstTarget != null) {
            fail("I could not find " + firstTarget + " on the screen. Please say the exact name again.")
            return
        }
        fail("Sorry, I did not understand " + phrases[0])
    }

    // ======================= AI voice typing =======================

    private fun beginVoiceFlow() {
        if (voiceActive) {
            stopVoiceTyping()
            return
        }
        val name = prefsObj().getString("voice_lang_name", null)
        if (name == null) openVoiceLanguageMenu() else openVoiceChoiceMenu(name)
    }

    private fun openVoiceChoiceMenu(name: String) {
        voiceIds = listOf("continue", "change", "back")
        voiceLabels = listOf("Continue with " + name, "Change language", "Back")
        voiceIndex = 0
        mode = Mode.VOICE_MENU
        playSound(soundScreenChange)
        speak("Voice typing. Swipe left or right, double tap to choose. " + voiceLabels[0])
    }

    private fun openVoiceLanguageMenu() {
        voiceIds = voiceLanguages.map { "lang:" + it.second } + "back"
        voiceLabels = voiceLanguages.map { it.first } + "Back"
        voiceIndex = 0
        mode = Mode.VOICE_MENU
        playSound(soundScreenChange)
        speak("Choose your main language. Swipe left or right, double tap to choose. " + voiceLabels[0])
    }

    private fun activateVoiceMenu() {
        val id = voiceIds.getOrNull(voiceIndex) ?: return
        when {
            id == "continue" -> startVoiceTyping()
            id == "change" -> openVoiceLanguageMenu()
            id.startsWith("lang:") -> {
                val code = id.substring(5)
                val name = voiceLanguages.firstOrNull { it.second == code }?.first ?: code
                prefsObj().edit().putString("voice_lang_name", name).putString("voice_lang_code", code).apply()
                startVoiceTyping()
            }
            else -> goBack()
        }
    }

    private fun isKeyboardOpen(): Boolean {
        return try {
            windows.any { it.type == AccessibilityWindowInfo.TYPE_INPUT_METHOD }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    private fun findInputField(): AccessibilityNodeInfo? {
        try {
            val a = rootInActiveWindow?.findFocus(AccessibilityNodeInfo.FOCUS_INPUT)
            if (a != null && a.isEditable) return a
            for (w in windows) {
                val f = w.root?.findFocus(AccessibilityNodeInfo.FOCUS_INPUT)
                if (f != null && f.isEditable) return f
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // fall through
